### [1] [Embodied AI Fails the Mirror Test: How a Simple Mirror Breaks Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33010)
*Xuanchen Liu,Hejun Zhang,Boxuan Dong,Ying Wu,Heshuai Li,Wen Gao,Yonghong Tian,Weihao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RoboDojo-Mirror 基准，系统评估含反射场景对机器人操作策略的影响，发现多种主流策略普遍性能下降，且退化不能仅由镜面作为障碍物导致的碰撞解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受动物镜像自我认知测试启发，检验机器人策略能否在包含自身或物体反射的场景中可靠操作物理对象，揭示当前机器人操作策略对反射环境的潜在脆弱性。

Method: 基于 RoboDojo 构建 RoboDojo-Mirror，保持任务目标、成功标准和策略检查点不变，比较无镜基线、单镜、双镜和镜面桌面条件；评估五类策略，涵盖 Vision-Language-Action 模型、World Action Models 和 agentic 策略；使用几何匹配的非反射面板作为对照，并结合轨迹与接触分析，诊断反射附近抓取、推动进度及主相机遮挡下的物理目标交互；在真实机器人上用 π0.5 和 FastWAM 验证。

Result: 所有镜面条件均降低聚合成功率：三种镜面条件平均下降 10.8 至 56.7 个百分点，镜面桌面条件最高下降 62.5 个百分点；50–88% 的镜面特异失败未记录到机器人—面板接触，说明直接碰撞不能完全解释性能退化；轨迹和接触分析显示策略出现对反射物体的“幻影 targeting”和真实物体的空间定位错误；真实机器人实验在竖镜和镜面桌面条件下总体成功率也下降。

Conclusion: 多种机器人策略共享对反射环境的脆弱性，表明需要面向反射感知的具身智能评估，并将反射场景纳入机器人操作策略的鲁棒性测试。

Abstract: Can a simple mirror expose the limitations of today's robotic manipulation policies? Inspired by the mirror self-recognition test in animal cognition, we investigate whether robot policies can reliably manipulate physical objects in scenes containing their reflections. We introduce RoboDojo-Mirror, a benchmark built on RoboDojo that preserves task objectives, success criteria, and policy checkpoints while comparing a mirror-free baseline with single-mirror, dual-mirror, and mirrored-tabletop conditions. Across five policies spanning Vision-Language-Action models, World Action Models, and agentic policies, every mirror condition reduces aggregate success rates: averaged over the three conditions, the drop ranges from 10.8 to 56.7 percentage points across policies, and it reaches 62.5 points on the mirrored tabletop. Because a mirror is also a physical obstacle, we compare it against geometrically matched non-reflective panels; 50--88\% of mirror-specific failures occur without recorded robot--panel contact, so direct collisions alone do not explain the degradation. Trajectory and contact analyses instead reveal recurring behaviors consistent with phantom targeting of reflected objects and spatial mislocalization of real ones. Targeted diagnostics further characterize grasping near reflections, policy-dependent pushing progress, and physical-target interaction under main-camera occlusion. Real-robot experiments with $\pi_{0.5}$ and FastWAM also show lower overall success rates under the upright-mirror and mirrored-tabletop conditions. These findings reveal a shared vulnerability to reflective environments across diverse robot policies and motivate reflection-aware evaluation of embodied intelligence. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [2] [VidForce: Predicting Force-Aware Articulation by Observing Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32967)
*Rafay Mohiuddin,Jiaqi Chen,Marc Pollefeys,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VidForce 从第三人称 RGB 视频中估计人与物体交互的轨迹和力曲线，并利用少量仪器测量作为先验，在真实视频和 Spot 机器人操作中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可通过观察他人交互预判物体运动与所需努力，但具身智能体难以仅从视频获得这种物理理解；直接力测量虽提供监督，却依赖专用设备且数据量有限。

Method: 提出 VidForce：以可选的 2D 接触点击和文本物体-动作提示为辅助，从第三人称 RGB 视频联合估计交互轨迹与力曲线。方法将有限仪器测量学习为共享力水平和时间轮廓的物理原型先验，由视觉和语义线索决定原型贡献，并用几何与视觉-语言特征适配观测；联合条件流模型细化初始跟踪轨迹和力曲线。配对的人类与仪器夹爪记录提供监督，另训练扭矩模型验证公式可扩展到其他传感量。

Result: 在 Hoi! 数据集留出交互上，VidForce 达到 6.6 cm ATE、峰值力 MAE 4.81 N、RMSE 5.92 N、力曲线 RMSE 5.52 N；在真实视频中能稳定推断运动与力。进一步部署到 Spot 机器人，用人类演示估计的轨迹和力曲线驱动真实关节物体操作，并报告重复试验成功率。

Conclusion: VidForce 表明，借助少量仪器测量形成的物理原型先验，可从视频中可靠估计交互轨迹与力，并迁移到真实机器人操作及其他传感量建模。

Abstract: People can anticipate how an object moves and the effort an interaction may require by watching others. How can embodied agents acquire this understanding from video? Force measurements provide direct sensory supervision, but require specialized equipment and remain limited in quantity. We introduce VidForce, which estimates an interaction trajectory together with a force profile from a third-person RGB video; 2D contact clicks and textual object-action prompts are optional. Our approach makes limited instrumented measurements useful for interpreting human interactions through learned physical prototypes as a prior. The prototypes share learned force levels and temporal profiles across interactions of comparable effort, with visual and semantic cues determining their contribution to each observation. Geometric and vision-language features adapt this prior to the observed interaction, while a joint conditional flow refines an initial tracked trajectory together with the force profile. Paired human and instrumented-gripper recordings provide measured force references for supervising human video, and a separately trained torque model demonstrates that the formulation extends to other sensory quantities. We evaluate trajectory recovery and force estimation through baseline comparisons and controlled ablations on held-out interactions from the Hoi! dataset. On this evaluation subset, VidForce achieves 6.6 cm ATE, peak-force MAE and RMSE of 4.81 N and 5.92 N, respectively, and force-profile RMSE of 5.52 N. On real-world videos, VidForce infers motion and force reliably and consistently despite the variability in human execution. We further deploy VidForce on a Spot robot, where trajectories and force profiles estimated from human demonstrations drive real-world articulated-object manipulation, and report success rates across repeated trials. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [3] [DOPER: Deep Object Pose Estimation Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32846)
*Hugo Hadfield,Alperen Degirmenci,Jonathan Tremblay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DOPER 是一个仅用 RGB 的 6D 位姿估计框架，通过共享视觉主干联合目标检测、分割和关键点预测，并用 PnP 恢复已知物体位姿；结合合成训练、真实观测部分监督、标定多视角几何约束和热图细化，在多数据集上取得实时 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从 RGB 图像恢复已知物体 6D 位姿时，需要减少对深度输入和学习式位姿细化器的依赖，并解决合成到真实域适应、模糊关键点对应以及不完整 CAD 模型表示等问题。

Method: 采用共享视觉主干联合检测、分割和关键点预测；从合成数据训练开始，利用真实观测的部分监督并保留合成关键点标签进行适应；使用标定多视角观测提供关键点监督的几何约束；推理时可选热图细化，将刚性位姿直接拟合到预测关键点分布以解决模糊对应；从欠定义 CAD 模型逐步细化物体表示和位姿估计器。

Result: 在 SPEED+ 和 SHIRT 上验证了对光照变化的有效适应和准确时序位姿估计；在 HANDAL、HOPEv2、HOT3D 和 T-LESS 的单视角与多视角配置中取得 SOTA 检测 AP；仅使用 RGB，并在单块 GB300 上以每个物体模型 35 Hz 实时运行。

Conclusion: 联合学习目标检测与位姿关键点，并结合真实观测和几何约束，是提升 RGB 6D 位姿估计有效性的关键。

Abstract: We present DOPER, an RGB pose estimation framework that shares a visual backbone across object detection, segmentation, and keypoint prediction to recover the 6D pose of known objects through Perspective-n-Point. Starting from synthetic training, DOPER adapts to real observations using partial supervision while retaining synthetic keypoint labels. Calibrated multi-view observations provide geometric constraints for keypoint supervision. During inference, optional heatmap refinement fits a rigid pose directly to the predicted keypoint distributions, resolving ambiguous correspondences without depth input or a learned pose refiner. Starting from underspecified CAD models, DOPER progressively refines the object representation and pose estimator. Results on SPEED+ and SHIRT further demonstrate effective adaptation to illumination changes and accurate temporal pose estimation. Across the evaluated single-view and multi-view configurations, DOPER achieves state-of-the-art detection AP results on HANDAL, HOPEv2, HOT3D, and T-LESS while using only RGB and running in real time (35 Hz per object model on one GB300). These results demonstrate the effectiveness of jointly learning detection and pose keypoints, with real observations and geometric constraints.

</details>


### [4] [LaWanle: Why Can't Leading Driving Models Make a Simple Turn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32650)
*Chengzhi Gao,Junzhe Gao,Yongzhi Liu,Weichao Zhuang,Weilong Hu,Taonian Liang,Guodong Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文揭示端到端驾驶模型在NAVSIM上接近人类，却在真实感闭环仿真中无法稳定完成简单90度转弯，并构建LaWanle基准及闭环DAgger微调来诊断和改进该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注现有端到端驾驶模型在开环/准闭环基准高分与实际闭环转弯能力之间的显著差距，尤其是同一模型在不同起点下通过/失败不一致，说明其闭环鲁棒性和路口行为仍存在隐患。

Method: 构建LaWanle转弯基准，取自三个真实感闭环仿真器，并从多个起点驱动同一转弯；通过路口入口微小旋转和航向修正分析失败原因；随后对开源车道保持模型在横向路径上使用闭环DAgger进行后训练，并比较恢复数据微调与等时长对照训练的效果。

Result: 仅训练5%参数即可将转弯通过率从19%提升至76%，且增益可迁移到另外两个基准并达到领先模型水平；但在路口入口旋转几度即可使原本通过的转弯失败，轻微航向修正可挽救约一半失败转弯；恢复数据微调只能修复训练中出现的偏差，与等时长对照相比在三个基准上均未提升转弯表现，所有变体仍远低于人类驾驶员。

Conclusion: 高分基准表现不足以代表闭环驾驶能力，简单转弯即可暴露端到端模型的脆弱性；小规模闭环DAgger后训练能显著改善转弯通过率并具迁移性，但恢复数据微调并非充分解决方案，模型与人类驾驶员之间仍有明显差距。

Abstract: We were surprised to find that leading end-to-end driving models across major paradigms, despite scoring close to human drivers on NAVSIM, cannot reliably make a simple 90-degree turn in photorealistic closed-loop simulators. To investigate why, we build LaWanle, a benchmark of turns drawn from three such simulators. Driving each turn from several starts, we find that the same model passes it from one start and fails it from another, and that the two drives look alike until they enter the junction. Rotating the car by a few degrees at the junction entry can fail a turn it otherwise passes. Conversely, a slight heading correction to the plans can rescue about half of the failed turns we tested. Such errors arise when a model drives by itself, so we post-train an open-source lane-keeping model with closed-loop DAgger on its lateral path. Training 5% of its parameters is enough to raise its turn pass rate from 19% to 76%. The gain transfers to the two other benchmarks, where the policy also scores among the leading models. Further fine-tuning on recovery data repairs the deviations it is trained on but, against a control trained equally long, improves turns on none of the three benchmarks, and every variant stays far below a human driver.

</details>


### [5] [ICLWorld: Benchmarking In-Context Learning of Agents from Digital to Physical Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32649)
*Qingle Liu,Jieming Li,Pei-lin Li,Shenghan Zheng,Ruize Yang,Sunqi Fan,Deyao Hong,Huan-ang Gao,Meng-Hao Guo,Shuojin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出在线基准 ICLWorld 与统一执行框架 UIverse，系统评估智能体在电脑使用和机器人使用中从上下文学习规则的能力，揭示数字世界与物理世界、规则识别与规则执行之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文学习基准多为离线、基于文本或静态图像，存在训练数据污染风险，且机器人评测多局限于方法级消融，无法充分评估前沿智能体在真实数字与物理环境中的上下文学习能力。

Method: 构建覆盖电脑使用与机器人使用的在线基准 ICLWorld，并提出将环境统一转化为用户界面的 UIverse；包含30个环境、630段演示视频（10小时以上）、160个带定制且常反直觉规则的任务。每个任务提供文本或视频形式的3个规则版本，并在回放演示、新实例和未见环境三个层级测试泛化；评测6个前沿模型。

Result: Claude Opus 5.5 与 GPT-6 Astra 在电脑使用运行中成功率为82%和76%，在机器人使用中仅27%和26%；无规则对照成功率最高仅8%。GPT-6 Astra 在三种规则版本下仅38%的电脑使用任务成功，为单一规则版本成功率的一半；机器人规则问答正确率达98%，但任务完成率仅20%。前沿智能体从视频和文本学习规则的效果相当并可跨环境迁移，较弱智能体依赖文本，且在演示情境重采样后视频学习成功大幅下降。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当前智能体在数字世界与物理世界之间、以及在识别规则与依据规则行动之间存在实质性差距；ICLWorld/UIverse 为在线、跨数字与物理世界的上下文学习评测提供了统一手段。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) lets agents acquire new rules from context and adapt to user-specific requirements at inference time. The same frontier agents now operate both computers and robots, yet their ICL remains insufficiently assessed: existing benchmarks are largely offline, rely on text or static images, and risk training-data contamination, while robot evaluations are limited to method-level ablations. We present ICLWorld, an online benchmark that evaluates the ICL of agents from digital to physical worlds, covering computer use and robot use, together with UIverse, a unified harness that turns every environment into a user interface. We build 30 environments, record 630 demonstration videos (10+ hours), and design 160 tasks with customized, often counter-intuitive rules that cannot be satisfied without learning from the context. Each task comes with three rule versions, given as text or video, and we test generalization at three levels, from replaying the demonstration to new instances and unseen environments. Across six frontier models, the strongest agents, Claude Opus 5.5 and GPT-6 Astra, succeed on 82% and 76% of computer-use runs but only 27% and 26% of robot-use runs, and a no-rule control reduces success to at most 8%. GPT-6 Astra succeeds under all three rule versions on only 38% of computer-use tasks, half its rate under a single rule, and answers 98% of robot rule questions correctly yet completes only 20% of these tasks. Frontier agents learn rules comparably well from video and from text and transfer them across environments, whereas weaker agents rely on text and lose most of their video-based success once the demonstrated situation is resampled. These findings reveal substantial gaps between the digital and the physical world and between identifying a rule and acting on it.

</details>


### [6] [GOLD-SLAM: Gaussian-splatting Omnidirectional LiDAR-Depth SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32335)
*Weicong Zhang,Yuan Huang,Yu Zheng,Lin Chenxiao,Yingying Li,Lilong Fan,Liangdong Zhuang,Yufan Chen,Ruiping Liu,Junwei Zheng,Jiaming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GOLD-SLAM 是一种全景高斯泼溅 SLAM 系统，融合 LiDAR 深度先验，在球面域完成跟踪与建图，并利用无 LiDAR 覆盖区域的视觉特征提升位姿估计；结合 DAEP 模块与 WHOLE 数据集，实现高质量渲染和更准确的几何重建，并验证了在 Jetson Orin NX 上的在线部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景相机可单帧覆盖近整个球面视场，但其视场与 LiDAR 视场不匹配，给依赖 LiDAR 深度先验的全景高斯泼溅 SLAM 带来显著挑战；同时增量建图中新观测表面覆盖不足和漂浮伪影问题突出，且缺少覆盖弱几何、高动态、全向、低光照等场景的评测数据。

Method: 提出 GOLD-SLAM，在全景球面域进行跟踪与建图，并使用 LiDAR 未覆盖区域的跟踪视觉特征辅助位姿估计；引入 Depth-Aware Map Extension and Pruning（DAEP）处理新观测表面覆盖不足与漂浮伪影；构建基于 UAV 平台的 WHOLE 数据集，涵盖 Weak-geometry、High-dynamic、Omnidirectional、Low-light 和 Easy 场景；并在 Jetson Orin NX（16 GB）上评估嵌入式配置。

Result: 在公开基准和 WHOLE 数据集上，GOLD-SLAM 相比透视系统具有竞争力的渲染质量和更高的几何精度；相比现有全景基线，平均 PSNR 提升 5.56–9.10 dB，平均 Depth-L1 降低 71.9–83.9%；嵌入式配置可在 Jetson Orin NX（16 GB）上在线运行，但会牺牲重建质量。

Conclusion: GOLD-SLAM 通过球面域 LiDAR-深度高斯泼溅 SLAM、视觉特征辅助位姿和 DAEP 策略，有效缓解全景与 LiDAR 视场不匹配及增量建图伪影问题，在渲染质量和几何精度上优于相关基线，并展示了资源受限平台上的部署可行性与质量-效率权衡。

Abstract: Panoramic cameras capture nearly the entire spherical field of view in a single frame. However, the mismatch between this panoramic coverage and the field of view of LiDAR poses a substantial challenge for panoramic Gaussian splatting SLAM that relies on LiDAR depth priors. We introduce GOLD-SLAM, a Gaussian-splatting Omnidirectional LiDAR-Depth SLAM system that combines high-quality rendering with accurate geometric reconstruction. It performs tracking and mapping in the spherical domain and uses tracked visual features in regions without LiDAR coverage to improve pose estimation. To address insufficient coverage of newly observed surfaces and floating artifacts during incremental mapping, we introduce Depth-Aware Map Extension and Pruning (DAEP). To enable broader evaluation, we collect a new dataset, WHOLE, using a UAV platform across Weak-geometry, High-dynamic, Omnidirectional, Low-light, and Easy scenarios. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks and WHOLE demonstrate competitive rendering quality and improved geometric accuracy relative to perspective systems. Compared with existing panoramic baselines, GOLD-SLAM improves mean PSNR by 5.56-9.10 dB and reduces mean Depth-L1 by 71.9-83.9%. To assess deployment feasibility on resource-constrained platforms such as UAVs, we evaluate an embedded configuration of GOLD-SLAM on a Jetson Orin NX (16 GB), demonstrating online operation at the cost of reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [7] [Seeing Is Not Reaching: Instruction Shortcuts Override Visual Grounding in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32206)
*Qing Zhang,Xuesong Li,Zhaoyuan Yang,Changlin Song,Xinyu Tian,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLA模型虽具备视觉定位能力，但在物体移动后仍常到达训练位置；作者识别出场景捷径和指令捷径，并提出无需训练的PSG来增强定位、抑制捷径，从而提升移动目标到达率和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA被期望继承视觉语言模型的视觉定位能力，但实际在物体被移动后仍倾向于到达其训练位置。作者在六个VLA中发现两类捷径：场景捷径使机械臂按原始任务场景行动，指令捷径使指令检索到所请求物体的训练位置。在π0.5中，即使物体名称注意力跟随移动目标，指令捷径仍存在，即“Seeing Is Not Reaching”。核心问题是：这种定位能力是未被使用，还是被捷径覆盖。

Method: 作者分别掩蔽图像介导的定位通路和直接的指令到动作通路来区分两者作用：掩蔽定位通路会消除对移动目标的到达，而掩蔽直接通路会增加对移动目标的到达，说明定位已能引导动作，但常被通过直接通路起作用的指令捷径覆盖。基于此提出Pathway-Separated Guidance（PSG），在不训练、不使用外部目标位置的情况下增强定位并抑制捷径。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO Object的Position扰动设置下，PSG将目标到达率从22.0%提升到52.6%，任务成功率从16.6%提升到40.8%，同时在原始任务上基本保持性能。

Conclusion: VLA的视觉定位并非缺失，而是常被指令捷径覆盖；通过分离并调节定位通路与直接指令到动作通路，可在不额外训练的情况下显著改善物体位置变化下的鲁棒性和任务表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are expected to inherit visual grounding from vision-language models, yet often reach for an object's training location after it has been moved. Across six VLAs, we identify two shortcuts: a scene shortcut, where the arm follows the scene's original task despite a new instruction, and an instruction shortcut, where the instruction retrieves the requested object's training location. In $\pi_{0.5}$, the instruction shortcut persists even though object-name attention follows the moved target. We call this Seeing Is Not Reaching. To determine whether this grounding is unused or overridden, we separately mask an image-mediated grounding pathway and a direct instruction-to-action pathway. Masking the grounding pathway eliminates reaches to moved targets, whereas masking the direct pathway increases them. Thus, grounding already guides action but is often overridden by the instruction shortcut, which acts through the direct pathway. We introduce Pathway-Separated Guidance (PSG), which amplifies grounding and suppresses the shortcut without training or external target locations. On LIBERO-PRO Object under Position perturbations, PSG raises the target-reaching rate from 22.0% to 52.6% and task success from 16.6% to 40.8%, while largely preserving performance on the original tasks.

</details>


### [8] [RoboProbe: Benchmarking Physical Plausibility of Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32152)
*Zhuoying Yang,Jiachen Ni,Yichen Guo,Xiaoshan Wu,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一套面向机器人世界模型生成视频的物理合理性评估方法：构建五类定律特定指标，并给出RoboProbe代理式评估框架与RP-Bench基准，用于检测生成视频中的物理违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成视频被用于真实执行前的动作提议与可行性/安全性评估，但当前视频生成器常产生物理上不合理的交互；仅依赖VLM的高层语义理解不足以完成需要时空证据支撑的物理验证，未检测到的违规会导致错误判断。

Method: 将分割、跟踪、3D几何或外观比较等时空证据与针对性VLM推理结合，定义Contact fidelity、Object persistence、Solidity、Robot deformation、Object deformation五个指标；提出RoboProbe代理式框架，协调指标执行并通过受限动作选择性细化评估；构建RP-Bench，包含来自12个机器人数据集、239个操作任务的761个人工标注生成视频和130个真实演示。

Result: 在RP-Bench上，五个指标均达到AUROC高于0.85，并优于所评估的学习型评判器、先前评估器及仅用帧的Gemini基线；将指标应用于五个视频生成器，揭示不同模型在物理违规类型和频率上的明显差异模式。

Conclusion: 该工作为机器人世界模型生成视频提供了可证据化、可细化的物理合理性评估框架，能够有效检测物理违规并揭示不同生成模型的特定失效模式，从而支持更可靠的机器人规划评估。

Abstract: Evaluating the physical plausibility of generated videos is critical for reliable robotic planning with world models. These videos inform action proposal and assessment before real-world execution, so undetected physical violations can lead to erroneous assessments of action feasibility and safety. Yet current video generators often produce physically implausible interactions. Detecting such violations directly with vision-language models (VLMs) remains challenging as physical verification requires evidence-grounded spatiotemporal reasoning beyond high-level semantic understanding. We develop five law-specific metrics for evaluating physical plausibility by combining spatiotemporal evidence from segmentation, tracking, 3D geometry, or appearance comparisons with targeted VLM reasoning: Contact fidelity, Object persistence, Solidity, Robot deformation, and Object deformation. Building on this metric suite, we introduce RoboProbe, an agentic evaluation framework that coordinates metric execution and selectively refines evaluations through constrained actions. To evaluate the physical plausibility of robot world models, we further introduce RP-Bench, with 761 human-labeled generated videos and 130 real demonstrations of 239 manipulation tasks from 12 robot datasets. On RP-Bench, all five metrics achieve AUROCs above 0.85 and outperform the evaluated learned judges, prior evaluators, and a frames-only Gemini baseline. Applying these metrics to five video generators reveals distinct patterns of physical violations across models, in both violation types and frequencies.

</details>


### [9] [View Management with Gaussian Placement and Opacity Regularization for Online 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32025)
*Dong Hwi Kim,Park Sangwon,Chaehyeon Song,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作针对在线3DGS-SLAM地图因仅用关键帧训练和不确定前端深度而导致的稀疏视角过拟合问题，提出渐进式非关键帧映射更新、边引导高斯初始化与自参考不透明度正则化，在实时预算内同时提升地图外观与几何质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS与SLAM结合可在线构建高真实感地图并提供相机位姿和初始高斯，但现有地图优化沿用传统SLAM设计：只在用于定位的关键帧上训练，并用不确定的前端深度约束几何。由于3DGS地图参数达百万级而每个区域观测视角稀少，地图易对稀疏输入过拟合；同时每增加一个视角或高斯都消耗同一实时预算，难以直接解决。

Method: 从两方面改进：1）在关键帧维持地图结构的前提下，随着映射更新完成逐步引入非关键帧图像，并根据不同帧组提供的监督信息分配有限更新；2）用边引导初始化在图像细节需要处放置新高斯，以控制地图模型复杂度；3）提出Self-Referenced Opacity Regularization（SOR），利用累积渲染证据且不增加额外渲染开销，抑制持续出现在地图导出表面参考前方的 disputable 高斯的不透明度，以补充不确定前端深度。

Result: 摘要未给出定量指标，但作者称这些设计在实时预算内同时改善了在线地图的外观与几何质量。

Conclusion: 通过更充分地利用非关键帧监督、按细节初始化高斯并利用累积渲染证据正则化不透明度，可缓解3DGS-SLAM在线地图的稀疏视角过拟合，在实时约束下获得更好的在线建图效果。

Abstract: The realistic rendering of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has drawn interest from real-time applications that require maps to be built online. This led to coupling 3DGS with SLAM, providing camera poses and initial Gaussians for the map. However, the map is still optimized with designs inherited from conventional SLAM, which train only on keyframes selected for localization and regularize geometry with uncertain frontend depth. These designs overlook the model complexity of a 3DGS map: with millions of parameters but few views of each region, the map overfits to sparse inputs. Resolving this is challenging, as every view and Gaussian added to the map competes for the same real-time budget. We close this gap from both sides. To add views while keyframes preserve the structure of the map, we progressively admit non-keyframe images as mapping updates complete and allocate the limited updates across different groups of frames according to the supervision each group provides. To control the model complexity of the map, edge-guided initialization places new Gaussians where image detail requires them. To complement the uncertain frontend depth, Self-Referenced Opacity Regularization (SOR) uses accumulated rendering evidence, at no extra rendering cost, to suppress the opacity of Gaussians that persistently contribute in front of a map-derived surface reference. Together, these designs improve both the appearance and the geometry of online maps within the real-time budget.

</details>


### [10] [TACTUS: Unifying Surgical Action Understanding in Time and Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31956)
*Lurui Wang,Hao Ding,Chenhao Yu,Chenyan Jing,Jiru Xu,Ruoqi Zuo,Liwei Mei,Chang Liu,Lalithkumar Seenivasan,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出TACTUS数据集与基准，用于统一标注和评估手术器械-组织交互的时空理解，并引入参考网络TactusNet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术基准多以任务特定目标定义监督，未完整刻画动作语义、器械运动与组织交互之间的关系，难以支撑全面的时空理解。

Method: 构建统一标注框架，联合标注工具与动作身份、嵌套动作/交互窗口、器械特定关键点轨迹及组织区域跟踪；采用人工标注、点跟踪和模型生成空间提示的两级流程，重标注5个公开来源的141个视频，得到22,995个动作片段、290万帧，其中Gold 15,668个、Silver 7,327个；提出TactusNet参考网络，基于共享视频特征评测语义、时间和空间预测，并与13个已发表基线比较。

Result: 在TactusNet与13个基线中，采用逐头编码器微调时工具-动作准确率达95.8%；最佳时间召回率为46.6%（时间IoU 0.7），接触Dice为0.351；加入Silver后，默认TactusNet的配对准确率从93.8%提升至95.5%。

Conclusion: 该工作为学习和评估未来手术自动化所依赖的器械-组织交互奠定了基础。

Abstract: Advancing surgical automation requires a comprehensive spatio-temporal understanding of how instruments act on tissue throughout an action. Existing surgical benchmarks, however, largely define supervision through task-specific targets, leaving the relationships between action semantics, instrument motion, and tissue interaction incompletely specified. To address this limitation, we introduce TACTUS (Tool, Action, Contact and Trajectory Understanding in Surgery), a new dataset and benchmark that establishes a unified annotation framework for complete surgical actions. The framework jointly specifies tool and action identities, nested action and interaction windows, instrument-specific landmark trajectories, and tissue-region tracks. To construct these annotations at scale, we develop a two-tier pipeline combining human annotation, point tracking, and model-generated spatial prompts. Using this pipeline, we re-annotate 141 videos from five public sources, producing 22,995 action clips spanning 2.9 million frames. The dataset comprises 15,668 Gold clips with human spatial prompts followed by review and re-checking, and 7,327 Silver clips combining human semantic and temporal annotations with model-generated spatial prompts. We further introduce TactusNet, the companion reference network, to benchmark semantic, temporal, and spatial prediction from shared video features. Across TactusNet and 13 published baselines, tool-action accuracy reaches 95.8% with per-head encoder fine-tuning, while the best temporal recall and contact Dice reach 46.6% at temporal IoU 0.7 and 0.351, respectively. Moreover, adding Silver improves the default TactusNet's pair accuracy from 93.8% to 95.5%. Together, these contributions establish a foundation for learning and evaluating the instrument-tissue interactions underlying future surgical automation.

</details>


### [11] [VLG-Drive: Unifying Vision-Language and Geometric Priors for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31952)
*Junge Zhang,Jie Zhu,Yanqi Jia,Xiangyu Xu,Zixun Huang,Wenbin He,Xin Li,Jiachen Li,Liu Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLG-Drive 将冻结的视觉语言模型与几何基础模型通过轻量融合接口结合，仅训练融合模块和规划器，从轨迹标签学习，实现相机输入下的闭环驾驶规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶规划同时需要 VLM 提供的场景语义和几何基础模型恢复的空间结构；作者希望避免微调任一基础模型，探索以轻量融合特征训练规划器的替代方案。

Method: 保持 VLM 与几何模型冻结，仅训练融合接口和命令条件规划器。在每个相机网格单元，通过几何锚定残差将配对的 VLM 与几何特征融合为联合 token，规划器读取这些 token 预测自车轨迹。

Result: 在扰动初始状态的 NavHard 上达到 31.78 EPDMS，为对比方法最高；仅用相机在 NavTest 上达到 88.27 PDMS。逐点融合在两个基准上优于独立 VLM 与几何流；在 420 个 HUGSIM 场景闭环驾驶中优于所有对比规划器。

Conclusion: 基于冻结视觉语言与几何基础模型融合特征的轻量规划器，是替代适配任一基础模型用于规划的有效方案。

Abstract: A driving planner needs both scene semantics, which vision-language models (VLMs) provide, and spatial structure, which geometric foundation models recover from visual history. We present VLG-Drive, which keeps both models frozen and trains only a fusion interface and a planner from trajectory labels. At each camera-grid cell, the interface fuses the paired VLM and geometry features into one joint token through a geometry-anchored residual, and a command-conditioned planner reads these tokens to predict the ego trajectory. VLG-Drive achieves 31.78 EPDMS on NavHard under perturbed initial states, the highest among the compared methods, and 88.27 PDMS on NavTest from cameras alone. Pointwise fusion outperforms independent VLM and geometry streams on both benchmarks. In closed-loop driving on 420 HUGSIM scenarios, VLG-Drive outperforms all compared planners. These results indicate that a lightweight planner trained on fused features of frozen vision-language and geometric foundation models is an effective alternative to adapting either model for planning.

</details>


### [12] [DUALingo: A Dual-Hand Instruction-Guided Dataset for Affordance Grounding on 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31919)
*Ming-Lun Lee,Yi-En Li,Feng-Dian Su,Ming-Han Lee,Kun-Ru Wu,Krzysztof Czarnecki,Yu-Chee Tseng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 DUALingo，一个面向任务条件、角色感知的双臂 3D 可供性定位大规模数据集，用自动化流程生成双合作角色热图，并验证其标注质量、模型训练效果与机器人操作可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 可供性数据集多只定位单一交互区域，而日常操作常需双手互补协作；人工标注双手合作接触区域难以规模化。

Method: 构建 DUALingo：将每个任务表示为两个协作且与左右手无关的角色，并在物体表面以热图定位；包含 71K 物体上的 271K 自由形式任务，由开放权重基础模型全自动生成、无需人工标注；用专家评分和经校验的 LLM 评判验证质量；基于该数据集训练现有 3D 可供性模型。

Result: 最佳模型可从自然语言指令定位两个协作角色，aIoU 相比零样本基线从 10.3 提升到 37.8，提升超过三倍；机器人操作演示验证了预测双臂可供性的可执行性。

Conclusion: DUALingo 为学习日常双臂可供性提供了可扩展基础，并支持任务条件、角色感知的 3D 可供性定位与机器人协作操作。

Abstract: Affordance grounding localizes where an agent should interact with an object to perform a task. Many everyday manipulation tasks, however, require bimanual affordance, in which two hands assume complementary and cooperative roles to accomplish a shared goal. Existing 3D affordance datasets primarily ground single interaction regions, and manually annotating dual-hand, cooperative contacts does not scale. To address this gap, we introduce DUALingo, a large-scale dataset for task-conditioned, role-aware bimanual 3D affordance grounding on diverse everyday objects. DUALingo represents each task as two cooperative, hand-agnostic roles, each grounded as a heatmap on the object surface. It contains 271K free-form tasks on 71K objects, generated by a fully automated pipeline of open-weight foundation models without human labeling. Expert ratings and a validated LLM judge confirm the quality of the resulting annotations. Trained on DUALingo, existing 3D affordance models learn to ground both cooperative roles from natural-language instructions, with the best-performing model achieving a more than threefold improvement in aIoU over the zero-shot baseline, from 10.3 to 37.8. Robotic manipulation demonstrations illustrate the actionability of the predicted bimanual affordances in performing cooperative tasks. These results position DUALingo as a scalable foundation for learning everyday bimanual affordances. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [13] [Beyond Expert Replay: Cross-Instruction Anchoring for Continual Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31903)
*Ziyang Chen,Dehua Chen,Shaoguang Wang,Xu Yan,Hao Huang,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨指令锚定（XIA），在有限经验回放下通过旧策略对旧指令的响应保持来缓解VLA模型持续学习中的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型需持续学习新操作技能并保留旧指令跟随能力；经验回放仅保留少量历史专家帧，导致许多旧指令响应缺乏直接监督，并出现旧指令触发后续学习竞争目标的失败模式。

Method: 在每个任务边界冻结上一策略作为教师，将旧指令与当前观测配对，并匹配教师策略对这些旧指令的响应，从而在不增加专家回放数据的情况下扩展对旧指令响应的监督。

Result: 在多种VLA架构和三个LIBERO套件上，相同历史专家数据预算下，XIA较经验回放取得更高成功率和更低遗忘；RoboCasa及真实世界实验进一步支持其有效性。

Conclusion: XIA通过指令条件响应保持补充经验回放，可有效提升有限回放条件下持续学习VLA模型对旧指令的保持能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models need to continually acquire new manipulation skills while retaining the ability to follow previously learned instructions. Experience replay (ER) can mitigate catastrophic forgetting, but retaining only a small number of historical expert frames per task leaves many old-instruction responses without direct supervision. Under limited replay, we observe a structured failure mode in which an earlier instruction can trigger a subsequently learned, competing goal. To address this gap, we propose Cross-Instruction Anchoring (XIA), which supplements expert replay with instruction-conditioned response preservation. At each task boundary, XIA freezes the previous policy as a teacher and matches its responses to old instructions paired with current observations. This extends supervision beyond stored historical examples without requiring additional expert replay data. Experiments across multiple VLA architectures and three LIBERO suites demonstrate improved success rates and reduced forgetting compared with ER under identical historical expert-data budgets. Additional RoboCasa and real-world experiments further support the effectiveness of XIA.

</details>


### [14] [G0.7: Scaling Up a Fast World Action Model with Long Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31822)
*Tianyuan Yuan,Baijun Ye,Shicheng Cao,Xing Nie,Xiaopeng Yu,Ke Dong,Zijie Zhao,Shenling Qiu,Yizhao Xu,Feiyang Xue,Haonan Liu,Haoyu Zhang,Haobo Jia,Zheng Liu,Anqi Yang,Jiahui Niu,Yue Sun,Tailai Cheng,Jianpeng Yu,Yang Wen,Longhao Wei,Zibin Dong,Shiduo Zhang,Yicheng Liu,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G0.7 是一个通用世界动作模型，通过将上下文窗口扩展至最多5分钟，并大规模预训练机器人遥操作与人类第一视角视频，在部署时跳过未来帧生成、直接解码动作，实现实时闭环控制并显著提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型虽能利用视频生成器的物理先验，但每次动作前生成未来帧导致测试时推理过慢；同时多数策略仅依赖较短观测历史，限制了长时程与泛化能力。

Method: 基于Fast-WAM，G0.7沿两个轴扩展：一是将观测上下文从单帧扩展到最多5分钟历史；二是将预训练扩展到大规模机器人遥操作和人类ego视频语料。训练时联合预测未来相机帧与动作以塑造策略表征，部署时采用Fast模式直接解码动作，跳过未来帧合成，并支持无暂停实时分块。

Result: 在公开仿真与真实机器人部署中，Fast模式与同一检查点的IDM推理性能匹配，单张RTX 5090上每16步动作块耗时60 ms，比IDM快7.6倍；性能随预训练数据一致扩展，人类ego视频带来最大OOD泛化增益。G0.7在五个仿真基准套件上平均得分70.8，在Galaxea R1 Pro和R1 Lite上零样本抓放成功率为48%和54%，视频条件上下文学习任务中ID/OOD成功率为96.7%和93.3%，七个真实世界后训练任务平均任务进度86.9%，包括多分钟折箱、饮品制备和产品装袋。

Conclusion: 结果表明，视频预测主要在训练阶段塑造策略表征，部署时可安全跳过未来帧生成；扩展历史上下文与异构视频预训练能显著提升世界动作模型的实时性、数据缩放性和分布外泛化能力。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) adapt pretrained video generators for robot control by jointly predicting future camera frames and actions, transferring physical priors learned from video into manipulation policies. However, generating future frames before every action makes test-time inference too slow for closed-loop control, while most existing policies remain restricted to short observation histories. Building on Fast-WAM, which showed that video prediction shapes policy representations during training so that future frame synthesis can be skipped at deployment, we present G0.7, a generalist world action model scaled along two axes: extending the context window from a single observation to up to five minutes of history, and scaling pretraining to a large-scale corpus of robot teleoperation and human egocentric (ego) video. Across public simulation suites and real-robot deployments, direct action decoding (Fast mode) matches imagine-then-act (IDM) inference from the same checkpoint while running in 60 ms per 16-step action chunk on a single RTX 5090 (7.6x faster than IDM) and supporting pause-free real-time chunking. Performance scales consistently with pretraining data, with human ego video driving the largest gains in out-of-distribution generalization: G0.7 achieves a 70.8 mean score across five simulation benchmark suites, 48% and 54% zero-shot pick-and-place success on Galaxea R1 Pro and R1 Lite, 96.7% in-distribution and 93.3% out-of-distribution success on video-conditioned in-context learning tasks, and 86.9% mean task progress across seven real-world post-training tasks including multi-minute box folding, drink preparation, and product bagging.

</details>


### [15] [LeanWAM: Learning Latent World Action Models from Frozen Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31740)
*Shaolong Li,Hongze Fu,Joyce Chai,Yinpei Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LeanWAM 提出一种轻量 latent world action model：先用探测实验证明互补的未来表征可压缩为少量 token 且保留大部分动作解码性能，再通过两阶段训练将冻结视频生成器的视频先验迁移到轻量策略中，实现高效且鲁棒的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM 通常基于大规模预训练视频生成器联合预测未来观测与机器人动作，训练和部署成本高；同时尚不清楚策略真正需要何种未来表征，以及这些表征能以多紧凑的形式表示。

Method: 作者先进行 probing 研究，发现互补未来表征可压缩到少量 token。基于此提出 LeanWAM：第一阶段用 Future Extractor 将观测到的 transition 压缩为 32 个 predictive tokens，这些 token 条件化冻结视频生成器，并受动作、RGB、语义和几何监督，同时从生成器隐藏状态的紧凑 readout 获得 complementary world tokens；第二阶段将两类表征蒸馏到策略中，使策略从当前观测预测这些表征并生成动作。

Result: LeanWAM 仅 1.4B 参数且无大规模具身预训练，在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus 和 RoboTwin 2.0 上优于强 VLA 与 WAM 基线；在 A100 GPU 上预测一个 action chunk 仅需 25.1 ms，比代表性 WAM 快 12.3–41.5 倍。

Conclusion: 紧凑的预测监督可有效把视频生成器先验迁移到鲁棒且高效的机器人控制策略中，表明策略并不需要昂贵、完整的未来表征。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) typically adapt large pretrained video generators to jointly predict future observations and robot actions, incurring substantial training and deployment costs. However, it remains unclear which future representations a policy actually needs and how compactly they can be represented. Our probing study shows that complementary future representations can be compressed into a few tokens while retaining most of their action-decoding performance. Motivated by this finding, we introduce LeanWAM, a latent WAM that transfers video priors from a frozen video generator to a lightweight policy via two-stage training. In the first stage, a Future Extractor compresses observed transitions into 32 predictive tokens. These tokens condition the frozen generator and are jointly shaped by action, RGB, semantic, and geometric supervision, while a compact readout of the generator's hidden states supplies complementary world tokens. The second stage distills both representations into the policy, which learns to predict them from current observations while generating actions. With 1.4B parameters and no large-scale embodied pretraining, LeanWAM outperforms strong vision-language-action (VLA) and WAM baselines on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin~2.0. It predicts an action chunk in 25.1\,ms on an A100 GPU, 12.3--41.5$\times$ faster than representative WAMs. These results demonstrate that compact predictive supervision can transfer video priors to robust and efficient control.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [16] [EvoMisty: Building, Optimizing, and Evolving Capabilities on a Social Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31341)
*Xiao Wang,Lu Dong,Ifeoma Nwogu,Srirangaraj Setlur,Venu Govindaraju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoMisty 是一个基于插件的框架，使编码智能体能够构建并持续改进社交机器人能力，并通过能力库与技能库实现任务优化和性能复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人任务多样且变化，现有基于 LLM 的方法虽能生成任务程序，但仍依赖人工开发的控制接口和预定义能力，且缺乏按用户指定指标优化已完成任务并跨任务复用性能提升的通用机制。

Method: EvoMisty 从官方文档构建控制程序，在物理机器人上验证候选能力；将验证后的能力存入原子能力库，并与已有技能组合完成任务；任务完成后收集执行数据、测量用户指定指标并迭代优化任务程序，最终将可复用技能蒸馏到技能库。

Result: 在 Misty II 物理机器人上使用 Claude Code 和 Codex 评估：分别构建 109 和 107 个经验证的原子能力，均完成全部 28 个 AutoMisty 任务及 7 个超出 AutoMisty 能力的任务；优化在 5 个任务上提升全部 7 项评估指标，对话响应延迟分别降低 52% 和 71%；复用优化后的对话技能使另外 3 个对话任务延迟降低 33% 至 72%。

Conclusion: EvoMisty 支持编码智能体从文档到物理验证、任务优化和技能复用的闭环，可持续构建和改进机器人能力，并显著提升用户指定指标。

Abstract: Social robots face diverse and changing tasks in companionship and education. LLM-based methods can generate task programs, but they still rely on manually developed control interfaces and predefined capabilities. They also lack a general mechanism to optimize completed tasks based on user-specified metrics and reuse performance improvements across this http URL present EvoMisty, a plugin-based framework that enables coding agents to build and continually improve robot capabilities. EvoMisty builds a control program from official documentation and verifies candidate capabilities on a physical robot. Verified capabilities are stored in an atomic capability library and combined with existing skills to solve tasks. After task completion, EvoMisty can collect execution data, measure user-specified metrics, and iteratively improve task programs. It then distills reusable skills from final solutions into a skill library for future this http URL evaluate EvoMisty on a physical Misty II robot using Claude Code and Codex. The two agents construct 109 and 107 verified atomic capabilities, respectively. Both complete all 28 AutoMisty benchmark tasks and seven additional tasks beyond AutoMisty's capabilities. Optimization improves all seven evaluated metrics across five tasks. For example, dialogue response latency decreases by 52% and 71%, respectively. Reusing skills from one optimized dialogue task reduces latency by 33% to 72% in three other dialogue tasks without further optimization. All code and experimental videos will be publicly released through the webpage: this https URL

</details>


### [17] [What Does a Skill Vocabulary Cost? A Closed-Loop Decomposition over Demonstrated Motions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32858)
*Maximilian Xiling Li,Nils Blank,Paul Mattes,Pankhuri Vanjani,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MPQ 将演示运动库置于已训练策略之后而非策略内部，在不训练参数的情况下把动作块量化为库中最近元素并做有界校正，从而把闭环失败归因到技能选择、放置与执行等可分离环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散技能策略把技能词表作为与选择器和解码器联合学习的隐式码本，闭环失败时无法区分问题来自词表、技能选择还是技能执行，缺乏可诊断性和可比较基线。

Method: 提出 Movement Prototype Quantization（MPQ）：在训练好的策略输出端接入演示运动库，将每个预测动作块替换为库中最近元素，并在限定范围内向原预测校正；整个过程不训练任何参数，可作为同一运动库上任意选择器的参考策略，并通过逐一替换选择器组件进行消融归因。

Result: 在包括 7B 视觉-语言-动作模型在内的六种架构上，经 MPQ 包装的策略与原策略统计等价。在 LIBERO-90 上，使用三种骨干网络的选择器时，量化本身无性能损失，参考放置与校正下技能选择最多造成 2 分下降；选择器自身的放置与校正解释了几乎全部剩余差距，最高达 12 分。离线重构指标无法预测这些差异，并会误判库大小、设计及跨基准套件迁移。

Conclusion: MPQ 提供了一种无参数、可诊断的离散技能策略评估框架，将运动库与选择机制解耦，使技能选择、放置和校正的贡献可被独立衡量；离线重构不足以评估此类策略，需以闭环表现为准。

Abstract: Discrete skill policies execute motions drawn from a finite vocabulary. In most current methods, this vocabulary is a latent codebook learned jointly with a selector and a decoder. When such a policy fails in closed loop, the failure cannot be traced to the vocabulary, to the choice of skill or to its execution. We present Movement Prototype Quantization (MPQ), which places a library of demonstrated motions after a trained policy instead of inside it. MPQ replaces each predicted action chunk with its nearest library element, corrects that element toward the prediction within a bound, and trains no parameters. Across six architectures, including a 7B vision-language-action model, the wrapped policy is statistically equivalent to the original. A reference policy executed through the library is therefore a baseline for any selector over the same library. Substituting a selector's components one at a time attributes its shortfall to them. On LIBERO-90, with selectors on three backbones, quantization costs nothing and the choice of skill costs at most 2 points under the reference's placement and correction. The selectors' own placement and correction account for nearly all of the remaining gap, up to 12 points. Offline reconstruction predicts none of these differences and misjudges library size, design and transfer across benchmark suites. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [18] [Atomic Policy: Composing Atomic Manipulation Skills with Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32813)
*Kaidi Zhang,Pengyuan Guo,Zhengtong Xu,Caiyi Zhang,Huayi Gou,Guanlin Huang,Aaron Lim,Anas Rafei,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Atomic Policy 框架，将 VLM 语义推理、PDDL 技能规划与技能级策略执行结合，通过语言/视觉指点和执行反馈实现长时程机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作要求机器人随场景变化将任务意图转化为物理交互。VLM 擅长语义与空间推理，visuomotor 策略擅长细粒度控制，但目标模糊和动作间依赖使二者集成困难。

Method: VLM 估计符号状态与目标，PDDL 求解器生成技能计划；agentic harness 通过语言、视觉指点或二者结合引导每个技能，并验证执行结果以指导阶段转换与重规划；统一的 phase-level 接口支持专用策略和通用动作模型。

Result: 仿真和真实机器人实验表明，Atomic Policy 提升了任务遵循能力，并能在复杂长时程操作中支持恢复与重规划。

Conclusion: 通过 grounded steering 和执行反馈组合学习到的操作技能，Atomic Policy 有效集成了 VLM 与 visuomotor 策略，增强了长时程操作的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation requires robots to translate task intent into physical interactions as the scene changes. Vision-language models (VLMs) offer semantic and spatial reasoning, while visuomotor policies provide fine-grained control. Yet ambiguous goals and dependencies between actions make their integration challenging. We introduce Atomic Policy, a framework that composes learned manipulation skills through grounded steering and execution feedback. A VLM estimates the symbolic state and goal, and a PDDL solver constructs a skill plan. An agentic harness steers each skill through language, visual pointing, or both, and verifies its outcome to guide phase transitions and replanning. A common phase-level interface supports specialized policies and generalist action models. Experiments in simulation and on a real robot show how Atomic Policy improves task following and supports recovery in complex, long-horizon manipulation. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [19] [MRBench: Benchmarking Physical Necessity Reasoning in Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32803)
*Yanzhe Chen,Zhijun Cao,Ruihe An,Chang Gong,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出物理必要性推理问题，并构建MRBench基准与MRScissors方法，用于异构多机器人操作中识别完成任务真正需要的最小可行机器人团队。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作成功并不代表所有参与机器人都必要；当机器人能力变化、资源不可用或被动物体可替代机器人支撑时，同一任务可能需要不同团队。可靠的多机器人操作需要理解任务需求、可满足需求的资源以及哪些资源真正必要。

Method: 作者构建MRBench基准，包含8个具有不同并发与可达性约束的任务，每个任务在5种配置下评估，这些配置在保持任务目标的同时改变所需资源。提出MRScissors，将任务需求与实测物理能力分离，并通过约束搜索识别最小可行团队。

Result: 评估显示现有协调智能体在参考团队下表现良好，但在机器人本体变化或被动物体可替代机器人支撑时表现困难。MRScissors在两个模型骨干上分别达到85.4%和91.2%的团队选择准确率，比最强基线分别高10.4和9.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了协调机器人与理解其物理必要性之间的关键差距，表明物理必要性推理对可靠异构多机器人操作至关重要，MRBench和MRScissors为评估与解决该问题提供了基准和方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in multi-robot manipulation have shown how robots can collaborate to accomplish complex tasks. However, successful coordination does not reveal which robots are actually needed. The same task may require different teams as robot capabilities change, resources become unavailable, or objects replace robotic support. We argue that reliable multi-robot manipulation requires physical necessity reasoning: understanding what a task demands, which resources can fulfill those demands, and which are truly necessary. We introduce MRBench, a benchmark for physical necessity reasoning in heterogeneous multi-robot manipulation. It comprises eight tasks with distinct concurrency and reach constraints, each evaluated under five configurations that change the required resources while preserving the task goal. Our evaluation reveals that existing coordination agents perform well with reference teams but struggle when robot embodiments change or passive objects can replace robotic support. To address this gap, we propose MRScissors, which separates task requirements from measured physical capabilities and identifies minimal feasible teams through constrained search. Across two model backbones, MRScissors achieves 85.4% and 91.2% team-selection accuracy, outperforming the strongest baselines by 10.4 and 9.5 points, respectively. Together, these findings expose a critical gap between coordinating robots and understanding their physical necessity.

</details>


### [20] [Supply Geometry, Learn Execution: Screw Attention for Zero-Shot Policy Transfer in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32797)
*Aly Magassouba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Screw Attention Policy，一种以几何为中心的操作框架：显式提供任务几何，仅学习执行策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模操作策略可能把大量容量用于隐式学习几何结构；作者认为这些几何信息可由感知和机器人模型显式提供，从而减少学习负担。

Method: 策略以物体位姿、相对 SE(3) 变换和关节螺旋轴为输入，这些由感知与机器人模型提供；仅需 30K 可学习参数，完全在仿真中单机械臂训练。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上达到 96% 成功率，布局变化下 85.8%，在五种未见机械臂上零样本迁移达 80.2%，真实机器人 24 次试验成功 21 次；无需适应或微调即可部署。

Conclusion: 操作策略在大规模学习中获得的大量能力可归因于几何结构，而该结构可以被显式供给，从而用极小参数实现强泛化与跨本体迁移。

Abstract: We present the Screw Attention Policy, a geometry-centric manipulation framework that supplies task geometry explicitly and learns only execution. The policy operates on object poses, relative SE(3) transforms, and joint screw axes provided by perception and robot models. The policy requires only 30K learned parameters. Trained entirely in simulation on a single robot arm, the policy generalises to new scene layouts, transfers zero-shot across robot embodiments, and deploys directly on a real robot, without adaptation or fine-tuning. It achieves 96% success on LIBERO, 85.8% under layout changes, 80.2% across five unseen robot arms, and 21 of 24 trials on a real robot. These results suggest that much of what manipulation policies learn at scale is geometric structure that can instead be supplied.

</details>


### [21] [Keypose or Dense? A Controlled Comparison of Action Representations for Stage-Structured Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32788)
*Haizhou Ge,Mengshuo Wang,Zhixing Chen,Lei Han,Chen Wang,Lu Shi,Guyue Zhou,Ruqi Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文比较模仿学习中的密集动作块表示与关键位姿动作块表示：前者每步一个控制目标，后者每步设为所属阶段端点并由限速伺服执行。实验显示关键位姿对示范扰动和物体变化更鲁棒，但对执行器与示范匹配更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可分阶段且端点可定义的操作任务中，关键位姿表示可能比密集表示更稳健，但其收益与代价尚不清楚，需要在匹配条件下系统比较。

Method: 在多个仿真操作任务和真实机器人开抽屉任务中比较两种表示；匹配块长度、示范、观测、策略架构和更新预算，并在调参场景选择各自执行规则；测试示范减速、暂停、改道，物体尺寸与位置偏移，干净示范稀缺性，以及三种策略架构。

Result: 仿真中示范被减速、暂停和改道时，密集表示成功率大幅下降，而关键位姿大致保持；关键位姿在物体尺寸和位置偏移下也领先。干净示范下，关键位姿仅在示范稀缺时领先，否则接近或落后于密集表示，前提是示范和执行规则适合其执行器；不适合时远低于密集。真实机器人上密集更常成功，关键位姿仅在执行器旋转和前进速度按示范调整后显著改善。

Conclusion: 关键位姿表示以对示范变化的敏感性换取对执行偏离示范的敏感性；其效果高度依赖执行器与示范的匹配。

Abstract: Most imitation policies that predict chunks of future actions use the dense action representation, in which the chunk holds one control target per step. In stage-structured manipulation, where a task can be divided into stages with definable endpoints, the keypose action representation instead sets each step of the chunk to the endpoint of the stage that step belongs to, and a speed-capped servo supplies the motion. We compare the two in several simulated manipulation tasks and on a real robot, with the same chunk length and matched demonstrations, observations, policy architectures and update budgets; in simulation, each one's execution rule is selected on tuning scenes. When the simulated demonstrations are slowed, paused and rerouted, dense success falls sharply while keypose stays roughly level, across three policy architectures and with endpoints extracted from the recorded demonstrations; keypose also leads under object-size and position shift. With clean demonstrations, keypose leads only when they are scarce and is otherwise close to or behind dense across the simulated tasks, provided that the demonstrations and execution rule suit its executor; when they do not, it falls far below dense. The same dependence on the executor appeared on a real robot learning to open a drawer from human demonstrations: dense succeeded more often, and keypose improved substantially only after its executor's rotation and forward speed were adjusted to the demonstrations. These results suggest that keypose trades sensitivity to such variation in the demonstrations for sensitivity to execution that departs from them.

</details>


### [22] [Simulator-as-Proxy World Model for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32786)
*Tingting Liao,Mingjia Huo,Hao Li,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric P. Xing,Guangyi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SimPWM：用规则模拟器作为可显式扰动的场景代理，由视频模型渲染，从而检验视频世界模型是否真正遵循模拟状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人视频世界模型常以模拟或渲染场景为条件，并假设生成视频会反映代理场景；验证该假设需要状态显式且可扰动的代理。

Method: 给定标定相机和首帧，SimPWM 用机器人3D模型与夹爪可能接触物体的3D点表示场景；无训练参数的规则模拟器根据动作更新状态，视频模型渲染结果，并同时返回真实感视频与模拟状态。通过冻结模拟物体而保留机械臂记录运动，检查视频是否仍显示物体被拿起。

Result: 在未见设置的DROID片段上，机械臂遵循代理：机器人IoU为0.75–0.78，高于端到端模型的至多0.69，且仅渲染机器人已足够。模拟物体提供物体状态：相较仅机器人代理，漏检抓取从54%降至38%；但视频是否遵循物体状态依赖基座模型。冻结刚性物体后，MiniMax-H3的拿起率从42%降至10%，而LTX-2.3仅从57%降至49%，后者似从机械臂运动推断抓取；MiniMax-H3剩余拿起中超过一半为布料和绳索，而模拟器将其刚性处理。

Conclusion: SimPWM的模拟状态能记录模拟行为；在扰动动作下，若视频与模拟状态不一致，即可揭示视频模型忽略模拟状态。不同视频基座模型对代理状态的遵循程度不同。

Abstract: Video world models for robots are increasingly conditioned on a simulated or rendered proxy of the scene, on the assumption that the video will show what the proxy shows. Testing this assumption requires a proxy whose state is explicit and can be perturbed. We present SimPWM (Simulator-as-Proxy World Model): given a calibrated camera, it represents, from the first frame alone, the robot by its 3D model and the objects the gripper may touch as 3D points; a rule-based simulator with no trained parameters moves them under the actions, and a video model renders the result. SimPWM returns the realistic video together with the simulated state. We then freeze the simulated objects while the arm moves as recorded and check whether the video still shows the object lifted. On DROID episodes from unseen setups, the answer differs for the arm and for the objects. With each of three base video models, the arm follows the proxy (robot IoU 0.75-0.78, against at most 0.69 for end-to-end models); rendering the robot alone already gives this. What the simulated objects add is the object state: against a robot-only proxy, they cut missed pick-ups from 54% to 38%, and the video follows them only with some base models: on rigid objects, freezing them lowers the lift rate of MiniMax-H3, fine-tuned on episodes whose simulation agrees with the real video, from 42% to 10%, but that of LTX-2.3, trained on the same episodes, only from 57% to 49%, as if it inferred the grasp from the arm motion; more than half of the remaining lifts of MiniMax-H3 are of cloth and rope, which the simulator carries rigidly. With any base model, the simulated state records what the simulation did; under perturbed actions, a video that disagrees with it reveals that the video ignores the simulated state.

</details>


### [23] [Risk-Informed Path Planning: Collision and Grounding Avoidance Application for Autonomous Surface Vessels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32760)
*Emir Cem Gezer,Ozan Kaya,Ingrid B. Utne,Roger Skjetne*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于贝叶斯信念网络（BBN）在线风险模型的RRT*路径规划器，用于海上自主水面船舶（MASS）的风险约束路径规划与可解释风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MASS在运营前和运营中必须证明安全性，因此需要在线风险评估来指导海上决策，并将风险纳入路径规划。

Method: 将BBN作为在线风险模型，评估危险事件的发生概率与严重度分布，并聚合为有界标量风险指数；在RRT*搜索中对候选边采样点评估该风险指数，优化由路径距离、航向变化惩罚和累积风险组成的复合代价，并施加风险接受上限的硬约束；每个搜索树顶点存储其状态下的BBN后验，使搜索树可作为个体危险的可解释风险地图；还定义BBN作为该场景风险模型的设计要求，并引入将重规划截止时间转化为单次风险计算时间严格上限的运行时模型。

Result: 在包含碰撞和搁浅危险的模拟港口入口场景中验证规划器；实测运行时间表明，规划器在运行时模型预测的单次重规划周期计算边界内运行。

Conclusion: 该风险知情RRT*规划器能够在给定运行时约束内完成在线风险约束路径规划，并借助BBN后验提供可解释的风险信息。

Abstract: Maritime Autonomous Surface Ships (MASS) must demonstrate safety before and during operations, necessitating online risk assessment to guide decision-making at sea. This paper presents a risk-informed Rapidly Exploring Random Tree Star (RRT*) path planner that integrates a Bayesian Belief Network (BBN) as an online risk model. The BBN evaluates the probability and severity distribution of hazardous events, which are aggregated into a bounded scalar risk index. During the search, this risk index is evaluated at sample points along candidate edges. The planner optimizes a composite cost function comprising path distance, heading change penalties, and accumulated risk, subject to a hard constraint enforcing a risk acceptance limit. Each search tree vertex stores the BBN posterior at its state, enabling the tree to function as an explainable risk map for individual hazards. We define design requirements for the BBN to serve as the risk model in this context and introduce a runtime model that translates replanning deadlines into strict limits on single risk calculation times. The planner is demonstrated in a simulated harbor entry scenario involving collision and grounding hazards. Measured run times validate that the planner operates within the computational bounds predicted by the runtime model for a single replanning cycle.

</details>


### [24] [Support2Sim: Visually Faithful Real2Sim with Stable Object Supports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32727)
*Samarth Chopra,Ankit Prabhu,Akshayan Nagarajah,Sumer Murthy,Pratik Chaudhari,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Support2Sim 是一个从单张 RGB 图像重建可仿真场景的 Real2Sim 系统，通过推断支撑图、接触引导的位姿初始化与物理 rollout  coarse-to-fine 优化，在时间预算内提升源图像保真度、支撑实现率和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理仿真场景重建不仅需要恢复空间布局，还需要恢复决定物体行为的支撑关系；设计目标是在实际场景生成时间预算内最大化源保真度、支撑实现与稳定性。

Method: Support2Sim 推断支撑图，并由接触引导的位姿初始化器为具有多个支撑的物体提出候选位姿，依据几何拟合与源图像一致性生成并排序候选；随后采用 coarse-to-fine 精化器，在受支撑物体间分配固定搜索预算，并用物理 rollout 评估位姿更新，接受更新后在新位姿处以更细的平移和旋转尺度继续搜索。

Result: 在覆盖四个数据集的 50 个场景上，与最强基线相比，Support2Sim 在 10 秒后源掩码 IoU 提升 21.4 个百分点，实现 87.1% 的预期支撑（基线为 73.2%），并将平均物体漂移从 79.6 mm 降至 45.3 mm。

Conclusion: Support2Sim 能有效从单张图像重建可仿真场景，并在实际时间预算内较好地平衡源图像保真度、支撑关系实现与仿真稳定性。

Abstract: Reconstructing a scene for physics simulation requires recovering its spatial layout and the support relationships that govern its behavior. We present Support2Sim, a Real2Sim system that reconstructs simulation-ready scenes from a single RGB image. Our design objective is to maximize source fidelity, support realization, and stability within a practical time budget for scene generation. Support2Sim infers a support graph, which a contact-guided pose initializer uses to propose poses for objects with multiple supports. The initializer generates and ranks candidates by geometric fit and source-image agreement. A coarse-to-fine refiner allocates a fixed search budget across supported objects and evaluates pose updates with physics rollouts. After an accepted update, it searches from the new pose at a finer translation and rotation scale. We benchmark our method on 50 scenes spanning four datasets. Compared with the strongest baseline, Support2Sim improves source-mask IoU after 10 seconds by 21.4 pp, realizes 87.1% versus 73.2% of intended supports, and reduces mean object drift from 79.6 to 45.3 mm. See our project page at this https URL for more details.

</details>


### [25] [AG-WAM: Attention-Guided World Action Model for Accurate and Efficient Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32722)
*Hongcheng Zhang,Lei Sun,Songxuan Liu,Tengteng Pan,Yuqiang Zhai,Xianglong Xu,Jianya Guo,Xiao Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AG-WAM，以显式注意力引导为统一原则，通过三个即插即用模块改进世界动作模型的内部注意力机制，在多个机器人操作基准上取得SOTA并提升训练/推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）虽联合预测未来观测与机器人动作，但注意力分配多为隐式学习，缺乏显式动作相关引导，限制了性能。

Method: 提出端到端AG-WAM框架，包含三个即插即用的显式注意力引导模块：内容自适应预测注意力模块、引导点视觉注意力模块和状态条件文本注意力模块，直接重构WAM内部注意力范式，且不依赖外部插件模块。

Result: 在LIBERO-X、LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin-2.0等公开机器人操作基准上达到新的SOTA，显著优于其他方法，甚至大幅超过使用大规模私有具身训练数据的方法，同时明显提升训练与推理效率。

Conclusion: 显式注意力引导可作为释放WAM能力的统一原则；AG-WAM无需私有具身数据即可实现更强性能与更高效率，验证了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: Recently developed World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict future observations and robot actions, providing predictive context for control. However, existing WAMs typically learn implicit attention allocation with limited explicit action-relevant attention guidance, leading to limited performance. To unleash strong capabilities of WAMs, we introduce explicit attention guidance as a unifying principle and propose our Attention-Guided WAM (AG-WAM), an end-to-end framework with three plug-and-play attention guidance modules that directly reformulate internal attention paradigm in WAMs: the Content-Adaptive Predictive Attention module, the Guidance-Point Visual Attention module, and the State-Conditioned Text Attention module. Without any external plugin module adopted, our end-to-end framework with the unifying principle of explicit attention guidance boosts the accuracy and efficiency of WAMs. Without using any private embodied training data, our AG-WAM establishes new state-of-the-art performance on multiple public robotic manipulation benchmarks including LIBERO-X, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin-2.0, significantly outperforming other methods, even those using massive private embodied training data by a notable margin, meanwhile notably improving training and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [26] [Seeing to Drive on a Single Board: Attention-Free Depth for Onboard Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32718)
*Itai Mizlish,Boaz Ben-Moshe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在 Raspberry Pi 5 与 Hailo-8L 上，通过蒸馏而非量化部署 Depth Anything V2，实现实时单目深度估计并支撑机器人导航栈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型地面机器人只能携带单相机和少量算力，而可把相机变为距离传感器的深度基础模型多为面向 GPU 的视觉 Transformer，难以在树莓派级整数加速器上高效运行。

Method: 先分析 Depth Anything V2 在 Hailo-8L 全整数算术下的量化失效：449 个 token 时，91% 的 softmax 权重低于一个 int8 步长，却承载 17-33% 的注意力质量。据此采用蒸馏而非量化，将 Depth Anything V2-Large 蒸馏为无注意力的 ResNet-34 U-Net 学生模型（24.5M 参数），并以 int8 部署；障碍测距仅依赖相机高度与地面交点，地图构建、hybrid-A* 规划和 SLAM 在 Pi CPU 上运行。

Result: 学生模型在芯片上达 27.1 FPS，int8 输出与 float 版本高度一致（Pearson 0.99993）；在仿真场地留出场景上 AbsRel 0.269，优于 0.354-0.530 的芯片上其他深度网络；在真实户外行驶的 1000 个立体验证点上与厂商 Depth Anything V2-Small 构建无显著差异，但更快（27.1 vs 21.6 FPS）。真实场地高斯泼溅重建的硬件在环测试中，10 个场景无接触到达目标 7.9±1.1 次，精确障碍物为 9.2±0.4 次；使用自身位姿与模拟轮式里程计为 7.7±0.7（10 个种子），真实 Pi 与 Hailo-8L 在环为 8.2±0.4（5 个种子）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，针对整数加速器蒸馏轻量无注意力深度网络，可在低功耗机器人平台上实现实时、准确的单目深度估计，并支撑完整的感知-规划-导航闭环；代码、预训练模型和数据已开源。

Abstract: A small ground robot can carry one camera and a few watts of compute, but the depth foundation models that could turn that camera into a range sensor are vision transformers sized for GPUs. We bring one to a Raspberry Pi 5 with the Hailo-8L, an accelerator that runs every layer in integer arithmetic, attention included. Quantised for it, Depth Anything V2 loses large parts of the scene, and we measure why: at 449 tokens, 91% of softmax weights fall below one int8 step while carrying 17-33% of the attention mass. Our answer is to distil, not quantise. An attention-free ResNet-34 U-Net student of Depth Anything V2-Large (24.5M parameters) runs at 27.1 FPS on the chip, its int8 output matches its float twin (Pearson 0.99993), and on held-out scenes of its simulated yard it is the most accurate depth network on the chip (AbsRel 0.269 against 0.354-0.530). On 1,000 stereo-verified points of a real outdoor drive it is not resolvably different from the vendor's Depth Anything V2-Small build, while running faster (27.1 vs 21.6 FPS). Ranging obstacles by where they meet the floor needs only the camera height, so mapping, hybrid-A* planning and SLAM run on the Pi's own CPU. In real-time hardware-in-the-loop tests in a Gaussian-splat reconstruction of a real yard, the stack reaches the goal without contact in 7.9$\pm$1.1 of 10 scenes, against 9.2$\pm$0.4 with exact obstacles, and in 7.7$\pm$0.7 on its own pose with simulated wheel odometry (ten seeds each; 8.2$\pm$0.4 with the real Pi and Hailo-8L in the loop, five seeds). The source code, pre-trained models and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [27] [Semidefinite Programming for Cold-Start GNSS Doppler-Based Alignment: A Comparison with Nonlinear Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32715)
*Baoshan Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出将模块化SDP对偶认证器附加到标准流形NLS求解器，用于GNSS多普勒初始姿态对齐，在保持原生协方差和低时延的同时获得接近直接SDP的全局精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部导航坐标系与ECEF坐标系之间的初始姿态对齐是多传感器组合导航联合处理全局与局部测量的关键前提。GNSS多普勒方法虽无需载波相位模糊度固定或多天线硬件，但其非凸问题用局部NLS易陷入局部极小，而直接SDP松弛时延高且不输出局部协方差。

Method: 在统一的极大似然多普勒目标函数下系统比较NLS与SDP，并通过蒙特卡洛仿真和三个真实车载数据集验证；进一步将模块化对偶SDP认证器附加到标准流形NLS求解器，利用拉格朗日对偶检验固定NLS候选是否达到全局下界，而无需调用完整SDP求解器。

Result: 该方法在0.001度以内恢复直接SDP的全局精度，将真实场景95百分位偏航误差从超过86度降至2.88至4.70度，并将中位求解时间最多缩短一个数量级，同时保留NLS原生的雅可比协方差输出。

Conclusion: 候选驱动认证能够连接局部估计与全局优化：SDP松弛扩大可行域且保持提升后的代价，使对偶检验可验证NLS候选的全局最优性；其非侵入式架构可直接接入现有导航流程，不替换局部估计器，并保留切线空间协方差和低典型时延。

Abstract: Initial attitude alignment between local navigation frames and the Earth-centered Earth-fixed (ECEF) frame is a critical prerequisite for multi-sensor integrated navigation, such as Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS)/inertial/odometry fusion, before global and local measurements can be jointly processed. While GNSS Doppler-based alignment eliminates the need for carrier-phase ambiguity resolution or multi-antenna hardware, solving this non-convex problem via local nonlinear least squares (NLS) suffers from local minima, whereas direct semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxation incurs high latency and omits local covariance outputs. This study systematically compares NLS and SDP on a unified maximum-likelihood Doppler objective across Monte Carlo simulations and three real-world driving datasets. We demonstrate that attaching a modular dual SDP certifier to a standard manifold NLS solver recovers the global accuracy of direct SDP within 0.001 degrees, reduces real-world 95th-percentile yaw errors from over 86 degrees down to 2.88 degrees to 4.70 degrees, and cuts median solve times by up to an order of magnitude while preserving native Jacobian covariances. We conclude that candidate-driven certification bridges local estimation and global optimization due to two structural advantages: (1) our theoretical comparison reveals that SDP relaxation enlarges the feasible region while preserving the lifted cost, allowing Lagrangian duality to verify whether a fixed NLS candidate attains the global lower bound without invoking a full SDP solver, and (2) the candidate-driven architecture acts as a non-intrusive module that plugs directly into existing navigation pipelines without replacing the local estimator, preserving native tangent-space covariance outputs and low typical latency.

</details>


### [28] [DeCoNav-Act: Benchmarking Collective Physical Capability in Multi-Robot Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32683)
*Yunzi Wu,Sunyao Zhou,Xiaotao Zhang,Yucheng Deng,Yuehao Huang,Lin Qian,Xuanrui Cui,Xinhai Li,Chenjia Bai,Hongqing Chu,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 DeCoNav-Act 基准，用于评估语言引导的多机器人共享物理环境移动操作与物理依赖式交接能力，并揭示现有方法在集体物理协作上表现很差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人团队可通过分担负载或组合互补本体协作，但前提是每个机器人留给队友可用的物理状态；现有多机器人基准多关注任务分配与技能排序，很少测试协作产生的物理状态是否可被队友使用。

Method: 构建 DeCoNav-Act：在共享物理仿真中评估语言引导的多机器人移动操作，包含350个同构和150个异构任务，均经联合参考执行验证；覆盖共享空间并行负载与物理依赖两类协调机制，其中一机器人需为队友建立可用条件；除团队成功率外，还测量路径效率、交接覆盖率与交接成功率，并利用已验证的交接前状态进行诊断评估。

Result: 评估 ASPIRE、RAX 的传感器落地适配版本及作者提出的 CoMoMa：使用 GPT-5.6-luna 时无方法完成任务，最多仅8.8%的依赖条件被达成，即使从交接前状态出发也无交接完成；使用 GPT-6.1-sol 时，CoMoMa 在26.5%的试验中建立条件，但仅4.1%成功。

Conclusion: 现有方法尚不具备可靠的集体物理能力，尤其在建立队友可用状态和完成物理交接方面存在显著瓶颈；DeCoNav-Act 提供了诊断多机器人物理协作关键缺口的测试平台。

Abstract: Robot teams can split workloads or combine complementary bodies, but only if each robot leaves the shared world in a state its teammate can use. Existing multi-robot benchmarks emphasize task allocation and skill sequencing, rarely testing whether the resulting physical states are usable. To evaluate collective physical capability, we introduce DeCoNav-Act, a benchmark for language-guided multi-robot mobile manipulation in a shared physics simulation. Its 350 homogeneous and 150 heterogeneous tasks, each verified by joint reference execution, span two coordination mechanisms: parallel workloads in shared space and physical dependencies in which one robot establishes a condition for a teammate to use. Beyond team success, DeCoNav-Act measures path efficiency and handoff coverage and success, and adds a diagnostic from verified pre-handoff states. Evaluating sensor-grounded adaptations of ASPIRE and RAX and our agent CoMoMa, we find that with GPT-5.6-luna no method completes a task, at most 8.8% of dependencies are reached, and even from pre-handoff states no handoff is completed; with GPT-6.1-sol, CoMoMa establishes the condition in 26.5% of trials, but only 4.1% succeed.

</details>


### [29] [DiffGR00T: Execution-Aware Differentiable Training for Humanoid Whole-Body Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32665)
*Sichen Wu,Qian Sun,Mengfan Liu,Weikai Qin,Bike Zhu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffGR00T 提出一种端到端可微的人形全身 VLA 训练框架，通过可学习世界模型将执行误差梯度反传至 VLA 动作头，在 HumanoidArena 四任务上平均成功率提升 12.5 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层人形 VLA 通常将高层动作生成与底层物理执行解耦，导致跟踪与平衡误差无法直接优化 VLA 生成器，难以满足人形全身控制对动态平衡和全身协调的要求。

Method: 将 GR00T N1.7 与预训练全身行为基础模型 SONIC 耦合；用动作条件学习世界模型替代不可微仿真器，使多步身体执行损失可穿过冻结的全身控制器和 flow-matching 采样器，直接反传至 VLA 动作头，实现执行感知的可微训练。

Result: 在四个 HumanoidArena 仿真任务上，DiffGR00T 将平均任务成功率从监督基座 VLA 的 59.2% 提升至 71.7%，平均提升 12.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: 执行感知梯度能有效对齐高层运动生成与闭环全身动力学，证明端到端可微训练可提升人形全身 VLA 的任务执行表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong results in arm-centric manipulation, yet humanoid whole-body control (WBC) requires dynamic balance and tightly coupled full-body coordination. Existing hierarchical humanoid VLAs typically decouple motion generation from physical execution, leaving tracking and balance errors unable to directly optimize the VLA generator. We propose DiffGR00T, an end-to-end differentiable training framework that couples GR00T N1.7 with SONIC, a pretrained whole-body behavior foundation model. To enable execution-aware differentiable training, we replace the non-differentiable simulator with a learned, action-conditioned world model, allowing multi-step body-execution losses to backpropagate through the frozen whole-body controller and the flow-matching sampler directly into the VLA action head. On four HumanoidArena simulation tasks, DiffGR00T improves average task success by 12.5 percentage points over the supervised base VLA (59.2% to 71.7%), demonstrating that execution-aware gradients effectively align high-level motion generation with closed-loop whole-body dynamics.

</details>


### [30] [DexWAC: A Unified World-Actor-Critic Model for Dexterous Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32654)
*Hongjia Liu,Dacheng Zhou,Minghao Fu,Chuanjun Guo,Tianze Wang,Qingtao Liu,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexWAC 是一种统一的世界-演员-评论家模型，在单阶段机器人预训练中联合学习演员、动作条件世界模型和自举评论家；它利用视觉预测与价值监督共享表征，并通过想象 rollout 或 critic 引导的 best-of-N 改进策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAM）仅把未来观测预测作为策略学习的辅助损失；多数策略改进方法又将动力学与价值分开学习，导致训练开销大且互补目标间表征共享不足。作者认为，视觉预测可促进控制相关特征，价值监督可促进任务相关表征，二者应联合塑造适合策略学习的表征。

Method: DexWAC 基于图像编辑骨干，对每个动作块预测其端点的视觉潜变量与本体感受，以及奖励和继续标志，使世界模型无需生成中间视频帧即可高效得到完整想象转移。在真实 rollout 上适配世界模型和 critic 后，通过 PPO 在想象 rollout 中改进预训练 actor，或用 critic 引导的 best-of-N 选择改进策略。

Result: DexWAC 在仿真中超过大规模预训练的 VLA 和 WAM 基线；在两个真实世界任务上平均成功率达 75%，而 π0.5 为 45%。其世界模型比基于视频的基线更准确且更快，消融实验证实联合学习动力学和价值能提升策略。

Conclusion: 将世界建模、价值学习和策略学习统一到单阶段预训练中是可行且有效的，可提升表征质量、世界模型效率以及真实机器人任务成功率。

Abstract: Robot demonstrations provide more than actions to imitate: they reveal how actions change the world and how those changes contribute to task success. Recent world-action models (WAMs) extend vision-language-action (VLA) models with the first signal by jointly predicting actions and future observations, yet this predictive ability serves only as an auxiliary loss for policy learning. Meanwhile, most policy improvement methods learn dynamics and values in separate models, increasing training overhead and limiting representation sharing between these complementary objectives. We introduce DexWAC, a unified world-actor-critic model that jointly learns an actor, an action-conditioned world model, and a bootstrapped critic in a single robot pretraining stage. Our key insight is that visual prediction encourages control-relevant features, while value supervision promotes task-relevant representations; together, they shape a representation suited to policy learning. Built on an image-editing backbone, DexWAC predicts, for each action chunk, the visual latent and proprioception at its endpoint together with its reward and continuation, so the world model alone efficiently yields complete imagined transitions without generating intermediate video frames. After adapting the world model and critic on real rollouts, we improve the pretrained actor through either PPO on imagined rollouts or critic-guided best-of-$N$ selection. DexWAC surpasses the large-scale pretrained VLA and WAM baselines in simulation and achieves 75% average success on two real-world tasks, compared with 45% for $\pi_{0.5}$. Its world model is more accurate and faster than video-based baselines, and ablations confirm that learning dynamics and value together improves the policy.

</details>


### [31] [BARE: Body-Aware Reflexive Evasion for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32642)
*Zihao Li,Tong Chen,Hua Song,Zitong Ding,Zihao Xu,Ce Hao,Weibang Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BARE框架，利用人类避让动作先验与强化学习，实现人形机器人快速局部避障并保持全身稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在真实环境中需躲避快速逼近物体；相比四足机器人全身大幅动作，人形机器人可利用丰富关节进行局部避让，以减少执行时间和所需空间，但局部调整会改变质量分布与支撑条件，难以兼顾快速避障与全身稳定。

Method: 构建由人类动作先验引导的反射式避障框架BARE：风险引导运动适配器根据受威胁身体区域从重定向人类避让动作库中选择参考动作，并按碰撞紧迫性调整时间相位；以适配参考和连杆级碰撞风险为条件，通过强化学习训练反射式避障策略，协调局部身体调整与全身稳定。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中验证，BARE能够执行有针对性的局部避让，同时保持全身稳定。

Conclusion: BARE表明结合人类避让动作先验、风险引导动作适配与强化学习，可使人形机器人实现快速局部反射式避障并维持全身稳定性。

Abstract: Dynamic obstacle avoidance is essential for safely deploying humanoid robots in real-world environments, where rapidly approaching objects require immediate evasive responses. Recent advances in reflexive evasion have focused primarily on quadrupeds, which typically rely on large-scale whole-body maneuvers. In contrast, humanoids can exploit their rich body articulation to evade through small, localized body adjustments. By moving threatened body regions without repositioning the entire body, these responses may require less execution time and less surrounding free space. However, local adjustments can alter mass distribution or support conditions, making it challenging to achieve rapid evasion while maintaining whole-body stability. To this end, we propose BARE (Body-Aware Reflexive Evasion), a humanoid reflexive evasion framework guided by human motion priors that enables rapid local evasion while maintaining whole-body stability. A risk-guided motion adapter is designed to select reference motions from a retargeted human evasive motion library based on the threatened body region and to adjust their temporal phases based on collision urgency, allowing the robot to reach evasive postures before potential contact. Conditioned on the adapted reference and link-level collision risk, a reflexive evasion policy learns through reinforcement learning to coordinate local body adjustments with whole-body stabilization. We validate BARE in simulation and real-world experiments, demonstrating its ability to perform targeted local evasion while maintaining whole-body stability.

</details>


### [32] [OmniAct: One Policy, One Million Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33158)
*Xiang Fan,Yuheng Wang,Jiayuan Yan,Bohan Fang,Wisdom Oluchi Ikezogwo,Ainaz Eftekhar,Max Argus,Jason Ren,Jiafei Duan,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniAct 是一个跨具身操作策略，通过以机器人运动学图为条件并预测各关节节点的相对运动，实现不同自由度与构型机器人的统一控制，并在仿真与真实机器人上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨具身迁移方法多依赖共享动作表示，但这种表示未必能刻画机器人形态差异应如何影响操作策略；单一策略需要同时迁移任务知识并适配每个机器人的能力与限制。

Method: 作者提出 OmniAct：将机器人关节位置、运动轴及与视觉观测的连接关系建模为场景相关的运动学图，直接作为策略条件；采用 flow-matching 策略为图中每个节点预测相对关节运动，从而支持不同自由度和运动学构型的机器人。为大规模学习结构对行为的影响，程序化设计并筛选一百万个体物理可行的机器人具身，开发仿真轨迹生成算法，并在多样仿真操作任务上训练。

Result: 在 2,000 个具身-场景留出评估回合中，OmniAct 成功率为 63.0%，对比 pi 0.5 的 17.8% 和最强基线的 57.9%；在 GPT-6 Astra 评估的任务族上达到 64.7%，高于其 41.1%；在先前未见过的真实机器人上，成功率接近次优方法 MolmoAct2 的两倍。

Conclusion: OmniAct 表明，直接以运动学结构为条件可让统一策略产生具身特定行为，并学习到利用不同形态能力、规避其限制的差异化操作策略，从而实现更强的跨具身仿真与真实迁移。

Abstract: A single policy that controls diverse robots must not only transfer task knowledge across embodiments, but also adapt its behavior to each robot's capabilities. Existing approaches facilitate transfer through shared action representations, but these representations do not necessarily capture how differences in robot morphology should shape manipulation strategies. We introduce OmniAct, a cross-embodiment manipulation policy that conditions directly on each robot's kinematics in a specific scene to produce embodiment-specific behavior. This kinematic graph represents the robot's joint positions, motion axes, and connectivity to its visual observation, allowing the policy to account for how its embodiment related to objects and surfaces in the scene. A flow-matching policy predicts relative joint motions for each node, enabling control across robots with different degrees of freedom and kinematic configurations. To learn how physical structure shapes behavior at scale, we procedurally design and filter one million physically feasible robot embodiments; next, we develop algorithms to generate robot trajectories in simulation for all embodiments; finally, we train across diverse simulated manipulation tasks. On 2,000 embodiment-scene-held-out evaluation episodes, OmniAct achieves 63.0% success, compared to 17.8% for pi 0.5 and 57.9% for the strongest baseline; on the task families GPT-6 Astra was evaluated on, it achieves 64.7% against GPT-6 Astra's 41.1%. On a previously unseen real-world robot, it achieves nearly twice the success rate of the next best method, MolmoAct2. Beyond task success, we observe that OmniAct learns distinct manipulation strategies that exploit the capabilities and accommodate the limitations of different morphologies. Project: this https URL

</details>


### [33] [PyRUA-Dex: Learning Programmatic Policies for Dexterous Bimanual Manipulation with Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33144)
*Feixiang Ruan,Guodong Lv,Kewei Zhang,Wenbo Huang,Yintianrun Zhang,Qiang Wang,Ruiyang Si,Shunyu Yang,Shulong Jiang,Weicheng Duan,Rui Ni,Jianxin Bi,Lekai Wang,Yuze Fan,Haobo Li,Zeliang Fu,Jiankun Zhang,Duomin Wang,Enze Xie,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PyRUA-Dex：无需高质量示范，利用编码智能体通过程序化策略学习灵巧操作，并在仿真与真实双臂平台上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作学习受限于高质量示范稀缺：人类视频重定向不精确、遥操作缺乏力反馈、外骨骼采集难以扩展。

Method: PyRUA-Dex 让多个编码智能体并行探索任务技能及其组合方式；蒸馏智能体将成功尝试归纳为任务库和一个程序。随后通过随机化布局进行细化，提升鲁棒性与执行效率。

Result: 仿真中在 Bench2Dex 取得 72.9 的 SOTA 分数，在完全随机化的 DexJoCo 上达到 81.0% 成功率，均优于所有已训练策略；真实双臂平台配备两只 22 自由度手，可高效学习弹钢琴、书法等任务，从乐谱学习新曲目最快仅需 30 分钟。

Conclusion: PyRUA-Dex 是在缺乏高质量灵巧操作示范时，利用编码智能体开发灵巧操作策略的有效框架。

Abstract: Learning for dexterous manipulation is limited by the scarcity of high-quality demonstrations: retargeting from human video is imprecise, teleoperation gives no force feedback to the operator, and exoskeleton-based collection is hard to scale up. We present PyRUA-Dex, a framework that enables coding agents to develop dexterous manipulation through programmatic policy, without the need of demonstrations. The programmatic policy is developed in two stages: during exploration, multiple agents explore in parallel for task skills and ways to compose them, and a distilling agent condenses the successful attempts into a task library and one program. During refinement, this program is improved through randomized layouts for enhanced robustness and execution efficiency. In simulated environments, PyRUA-Dex achieves SOTA on Bench2Dex with a score of 72.9 and reaches 81.0% success on DexJoCo under full randomization, ahead of every trained policy on both benchmarks. In real world experiments on a bimanual platform with two 22-DoF hands, PyRUA-Dex learns to play piano, write calligraphy, and other tasks efficiently: learning a new piece from sheet music takes as little as 30 minutes. These results suggest that PyRUA-Dex is an effective framework for developing dexterous manipulation policies with coding agents in the absence of high quality dexterous manipulation demonstrations.

</details>


### [34] [ROAM-Bench: Evaluating Embodied Active Spatial Intelligence through Evidence Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33143)
*Xingjian Ran,Xiaoye Mo,Sihao Liu,Li Luo,Taiqiang Wu,Ji Zhao,Wenqi Shao,Hongyang Li,Jiaqi Wang,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ROAM-Bench，用于在机器人运动与碰撞约束下评测具身空间智能，并区分“结果正确”与“任务要求完成”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准难以区分正确结果与任务要求是否真正完成；具身空间智能需要智能体通过探索与交互获取信息，并据此推理和行动。

Method: ROAM-Bench包含250个人工验证任务、11类任务、5个任务组和27个室内环境，覆盖物体/房间搜索、计数、几何判断、方向推理与重定位。利用仿真状态和回合记录进行独立检查，评估结果与任务要求，并用几何、覆盖或放置等更强检查验证过程质量。

Result: 在100个抽样任务上，人类评估者达到84%的严格任务成功率，且所有正确结果均满足必要检查。七个前沿视觉语言模型表现出两类互补失败模式：结果正确但未满足任务要求；结果错误但通过更强检查。Oracle输入诊断表明，更完整的目标观测与校准反馈可改善最终空间判断。

Conclusion: ROAM-Bench揭示现有模型在具身空间任务中结果与要求脱节的问题，强调需同时评估结果正确性和任务要求完成度，并为通过更完整观测与校准反馈提升空间判断提供依据。

Abstract: Embodied spatial intelligence requires agents to gather information through exploration and interaction, then use it to reason and act. Existing benchmarks insufficiently distinguish correct outcomes from completion of these task requirements. We introduce ROAM-Bench to evaluate these abilities under robot motion and collision constraints. Independent checks use simulation state and episode records to assess outcomes and task requirements, with stronger checks of geometry, coverage, or placement. It comprises 250 manually validated tasks across 11 types in five groups and 27 indoor environments, covering object and room search, counting, geometric judgments, direction reasoning, and relocation. Human evaluators achieve 84% strict task success on 100 sampled tasks, with every correct outcome satisfying the required checks. Across seven frontier vision-language models, we identify two complementary failure modes: correct outcomes with unmet task requirements, and incorrect outcomes despite passing stronger checks. Oracle-input diagnostics show that more complete target observations and calibrated feedback improve final spatial judgments.

</details>


### [35] [iWAM: Efficient Action Prediction from World Models without Explicit Future Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33141)
*Vansh Kapoor,Max Sobol Mark,Sreyas Venkataraman,Max Simchowitz,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: iWAM 将世界-动作模型的动作策略条件化于视频生成模型单次前向的中间特征，而非完整生成未来帧，从而在保留泛化优势的同时显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型依赖预训练视频生成模型预测场景演化以辅助决策，但完整未来帧生成对实时控制过于昂贵；现有高效代理方法又难以在不损失动作预测质量的前提下训练。
