Using Lie bracket computations and the strong Lie Algebra Rank Condition, it is shown that the full nonlinear dynamics are generically small-time locally accessible, with singular configurations explicitly characterized and shown to be immediately escapable by direct mass actuation. The stronger property of small-time local controllability is shown to hold for the attitude subsystem at equilibrium points, using Sussmann's condition. A key contribution is the explicit treatment of the time-varying inertia tensor via the Huygens-Steiner Theorem, which introduces nontrivial coupling between the control vector fields. The momentum wheel provides direct yaw authority that, combined with inertia variation from the moving mass, generates a full-rank accessibility Lie algebra. A nonlinear Model Predictive Controller (MPC) is derived with consideration of flight envelope constraints and is applied to track different trajectories under unperturbed and perturbed flow field conditions.

</details>


### [106] [Learning from Human Corrections through Temporal Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31446)
*Anjiabei Wang,Tesca Fitzgerald*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作研究如何同时利用干预前与干预后的机器人行为构建偏好对，以学习近似人类潜在奖励的价值函数，并验证其在目标推断与纠正预测中的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Learning from Corrections方法主要比较纠正后的新轨迹与机器人原计划行为，忽略了干预发生前后的上下文，尤其是“何时干预”所携带的信息。

Method: 将纠正后的行为与其替代的原行为比较，构建干预后偏好；利用干预前状态之间的时间关系构建干预前偏好；用这些偏好对学习价值表示，并在目标推断和纠正预测两个下游任务上评估。

Result: 干预后偏好对目标推断特别有效，干预前偏好对纠正预测表现强；结合两类信息在两个任务上整体表现好，且能泛化到训练中未见过的参与者。

Conclusion: 人类“何时纠正”和“如何纠正”提供互补信息，均可用于从纠正交互中学习价值表示。

Abstract: When people intervene to correct a robot, they provide information not only through how they correct its behavior, but also through when they choose to intervene. Existing approaches to Learning from Corrections focus on comparing the new trajectory to the robot's originally intended behavior, thus underutilizing the context of the robot's behavior before the intervention. In this work, we investigate how both pre- and post-intervention behavior can be translated into preference pairs for learning value functions that approximate a human's latent reward. We construct post-intervention preferences by comparing corrected behavior with the behavior it replaces, and pre-intervention preferences from temporal relationships among states leading up to an intervention. We evaluate the learned value representations through two downstream tasks: goal inference and correction forecasting. Our results show that post-intervention preferences are particularly effective for goal inference, while pre-intervention preferences provide strong correction-forecasting performance. Combining both sources of information performs strongly across both tasks, even when generalizing to participants unseen during training. These findings demonstrate that both when and how humans correct provide complementary information for learning value representations from corrective interactions.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [From Safety-Driven Redundancy to Value-Driven Architecture: An ODD-Based Rationalization Framework for Level 4 Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32960)
*Tiago Simão Ferreira,Farzad Fallahi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种基于运行设计域（ODD）、以安全为约束的系统工程框架，用于L4自动驾驶电子电气/系统架构的合理化设计；在安全可行设计空间内评估架构变换，并结合生命周期成本敏感性与多目标Pareto分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L4自动驾驶需在感知、计算、通信、电源和执行故障下保持关键驾驶功能，但面向工业规模化时，架构复杂性、验证负担、集成工作量和生命周期成本日益重要，因此需要在不牺牲安全的前提下系统化地精简和重构架构。

Method: 将ODD相关能力、物理故障假设、故障后运行策略和空间感知需求输入集成的安全可行性门控，先界定安全可行设计空间；仅在该空间内评估删除、迁移、功能重分配和合并等架构变换，随后进行生命周期成本敏感性分析与稳健多目标Pareto分析；以通用、合成参数化的L4架构作为演示性计算案例。

Result: 在该建模设计空间中，直接消融的合理化潜力有限，而方向性重分配与功能合并产生更多安全可行候选；在示例生命周期成本模型下，最佳标称候选实现5.55%的归一化成本降低，并在规定不确定性域内5,000个采样点上均保持降本；识别出15个非支配架构-策略方案，代表七类物理变换。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在所述假设下，基于能力、由策略条件化并考虑生命周期成本的架构合理化可行，且安全作为非补偿性可行性约束；所报告的变换属于设计候选，而非已验证的量产架构。

Abstract: Level 4 autonomous vehicles require fault-tolerant architectures that preserve critical driving functions under sensing, computing, communication, power, and actuation failures. As these systems progress toward industrial scalability, architectural complexity, validation burden, integration effort, and lifecycle cost become increasingly relevant. This study proposes an Operational Design Domain (ODD)-based, safety-constrained systems-engineering framework for architecture rationalization. ODD-specific capabilities, physical fault assumptions, post-fault operational policies, and spatial sensing requirements are propagated through an integrated safety-feasibility gate. Architecture transformations, including removal, relocation, functional reassignment, and consolidation, are evaluated only within the resulting safety-feasible design space, followed by lifecycle-cost sensitivity and robust multiobjective Pareto analysis. A generic, synthetically parameterized Level 4 architecture is used as a demonstrative computational case study. Within this modeled design space, direct ablation showed limited rationalization potential, whereas directional redistribution and functional consolidation generated a broader set of safety-feasible candidates. Under the illustrative lifecycle-cost model, the best nominal candidate achieved a 5.55% normalized cost reduction and remained cost-reducing across all 5,000 sampled points in the prescribed uncertainty domain. The analysis identified 15 non-dominated architecture-policy solutions representing seven physical transformations. The results support capability-based, policy-conditioned, and lifecycle-aware architecture rationalization under the stated assumptions, with safety retained as a non-compensatory feasibility constraint. The reported transformations are design candidates rather than validated production architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Perception Is Enough, at the Right Scale: Scale-Adaptive Token Pruning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31954)
*Yifeng Lin,Liuxiang Qiu,Yunfeng Chen,Tiesong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TauPrune-VLA 是一种无需训练的 VLA 模型 token 剪枝方法，通过感知温度 τ 自适应控制保留的感知表示尺度，实现最高 1.86× 推理加速并保持接近基线的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型需持续处理视觉流，计算开销限制实时机器人控制；现有 token 剪枝采用固定语义尺度，可能忽略操作不同阶段所需信息。作者发现，当选择尺度适应场景演变的语义结构时，感知语义可指导有效 token 选择，且无需依赖内部注意力。

Method: 提出 TauPrune-VLA：用单一感知温度 τ 控制保留感知表示的尺度；根据感知语义变化趋势在线更新 τ，并由机器人状态变化和动作幅度调节调整强度；利用相邻帧语义相对稳定，将当前观测计算的保留索引用于下一次策略调用，使 token 选择与推理异步执行，降低剪枝开销。

Result: 在多种 VLA 模型和仿真基准上，TauPrune-VLA 实现最高 1.86× 推理加速，同时任务成功率接近基线；并在两个真实机器人平台上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: TauPrune-VLA 表明，基于感知语义的自适应、无需训练 token 剪枝可在不显著损失任务性能的情况下提升 VLA 推理效率，并具备仿真与真实机器人场景的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models must process continuous streams of visual observations, and the resulting computational cost limits real-time robotic control. Token pruning reduces this cost, but a fixed semantic scale of perceptual representation can overlook information needed at different stages of manipulation. We show that perceptual semantics can guide effective token selection without relying on internal attention when the selection scale adapts to the scene's evolving semantic structure. Based on this observation, we introduce TauPrune-VLA, a training-free method that controls the scale of the retained perceptual representation through a single perception temperature $\tau$. To adapt this scale as manipulation proceeds, TauPrune-VLA updates $\tau$ online using trends in perceptual semantics, with robot-state changes and action magnitude regulating the adjustment strength. While these semantics evolve over the course of manipulation, they remain relatively stable between consecutive frames. This continuity allows retention indices computed from the current observation to be applied at the next policy invocation, enabling token selection to run asynchronously with inference and reducing pruning overhead. Evaluated across diverse VLA models and simulation benchmarks, TauPrune-VLA achieves up to a $1.86\times$ inference speedup while maintaining near-baseline task success rates. We further validate its effectiveness on two real-world robotic platforms.

</details>


<div id='physics.bio-ph'></div>

# physics.bio-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [The cyborg fly: reflexes survive engineered actuators, coordination needs stand-ins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31644)
*Mauricio Muñoz Arias*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将雌性BANC与雄性MaleCNS两个果蝇全中枢连接组分别构建为约16万神经元的泄漏积分发放（LIF）脉冲网络，并闭环接入模拟传感器、执行器和身体，检验连接组能否驱动行走与飞行。结果显示连接组可产生反射、节律和命令信号，但不能自发涌现六足协调行走、飞行稳定和扰动校正，需工程化替身补足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两性果蝇全中枢连接组图谱刚完成，关键问题是：将其作为脉冲神经网络运行能否驱动身体？若把肌肉换成工程执行器，哪些功能仍由接线决定？以及如何区分真实连接组贡献与手写控制规则造成的表观依赖。

Method: 用BANC和MaleCNS构建约16万神经元的LIF网络，闭环连接模拟传感器、执行器和行走/飞行身体；执行器为端口Hamiltonian模型，可逐步审计能量，替换执行器仅改配置文件。采用五级打乱（从全神经系统到突触计数）的null网络检验接线依赖性。测试糖在labellum驱动proboscis运动神经元、腿关节抵抗 imposed movement、腿电路振荡、looming诱导MaleCNS同侧起飞信号、下行神经元调节和操纵飞行；比较20种执行器设置（含Hill型肌肉对和齿轮DC电机），并重建已发表闭环以分析odor navigation来源。

Result: 接线产生特定功能：糖刺激驱动喙运动神经元，腿关节抵抗 imposed movement，腿电路振荡，looming在MaleCNS驱动同侧起飞信号，下行神经元调节飞行推力和转向。抵抗反射在MaleCNS的20/20、BANC的19/20执行器设置中保留；执行器类型主要决定行走速度和能量成本。六足协调行走、飞行稳定性和扰动校正未自发涌现，均需带标签的工程替身，连接组可对其发出命令。重建的odor navigation中，气味导航来自手写规则，网络仅通过一个valence数介入，说明工程替身可制造表观接线依赖。两性功能差异可追溯到接线差异，如MaleCNS中haltere对飞行动力肌的驱动。

Conclusion: 具身的全中枢连接组可提供反射、节律和命令信号，但不提供将这些元素组织成完整行为的协调机制；闭环行为分析需警惕工程替身造成的表观连接组依赖性。

Abstract: Two wiring diagrams now cover the whole central nervous system of an adult fruit fly: one female, one male. Can such a diagram, run as a network of spiking neurons, drive a body? And if we swap the body's muscles for engineered actuators, what survives? We ran both connectomes, BANC and MaleCNS, as leaky integrate-and-fire networks of about 160,000 neurons each, and closed the loop through simulated sensors, actuators and bodies for walking and flight. The actuators are port-Hamiltonian, so energy is audited at every step, and swapping one for another changes only a configuration file. Null networks shuffled at five levels, from the whole nervous system to synapse counts, test whether a result depends on the wiring. The wiring produced specific functions: sugar on the labellum drove the proboscis motor neuron, leg joints resisted imposed movement, leg circuits oscillated, a looming disc drove a take-off signal on its side (in MaleCNS), and descending neurons throttled and steered flight. The resistance reflex survived 20 of 20 actuator settings in MaleCNS and 19 of 20 in BANC, including a Hill-type muscle pair and a geared DC motor; the actuator set walking speed and energy cost instead. Coordinated six-leg walking, flight stability and the correction of perturbations did not emerge. Each needed a labelled engineered stand-in, which the connectome could command. Such stand-ins can also manufacture an apparent dependence on wiring: in a rebuild of a published closed loop, odour navigation came from a hand-written rule, and the network entered only through one valence number. The two animals differed on some functions, and the differences traced to wiring, such as a haltere drive of the flight power muscles found only in MaleCNS. A whole-CNS connectome, embodied, gives reflexes, rhythms and command signals, but not the coordination that turns them into behaviour.

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [One Phase Is Enough: Sim-to-Real Transfer with Direct Visual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31588)
*John Marangola,Antonia Bronars,Younghyo Park,Stephen Andrews,Kushal Kedia,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT 提出一种基于冻结预训练视觉编码器的一阶段视觉强化学习方案，在匹配条件下优于“教师蒸馏学生”的两阶段方法，并显著提升真实机器人操作成功率与分布外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉策略从仿真迁移到真实世界仍很困难。常见两阶段方法先用特权状态训练教师策略，再通过模仿蒸馏为视觉学生，但学生性能常显著低于教师；直接用视觉观察做一阶段强化学习又因从零学习任务相关视觉表征样本效率低而难以可行。

Method: DRIFT 使用测试时可获得的图像观察，直接进行一阶段视觉强化学习。其核心是采用冻结的预训练基础视觉编码器，使强化学习只需学习控制策略，避免对视觉骨干网络进行昂贵反向传播；同时利用预训练表征，使策略能够从快速、光栅化的仿真渲染迁移到真实世界。

Result: 在匹配网络架构、观察和计算预算的条件下，DRIFT 在三个操作任务上优于两阶段蒸馏方法，真实世界平均成功率相对提升 58.8%。受控仿真实验显示，其相对优势从同分布下约 9% 增加到分布外偏移下超过 61%，表明更强泛化有助于真实世界迁移。

Conclusion: 冻结预训练视觉编码器并直接进行一阶段视觉强化学习，是一种简单有效的视觉 sim-to-real 配方，可优于传统两阶段蒸馏，并带来更好的真实世界成功率和分布外泛化能力。

Abstract: Sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) has enabled impressive robotic behaviors, yet deploying policies directly from vision remains challenging. A common approach first trains a teacher policy with RL using privileged state observations, then distills its behavior into a vision-based student via imitation. However, the resulting student can substantially underperform its teacher. Directly training visual policies with RL offers a promising alternative, but learning task-relevant visual representations from scratch can be sample-inefficient, making one-phase visual RL intractable. We propose DRIFT, a simple recipe for efficient one-phase visual RL using image observations available at test time. Our key insight is to use frozen, pretrained foundation vision encoders, allowing RL to focus on learning control from existing visual representations. Freezing the encoder avoids costly backpropagation through the vision backbone, while its pretrained representations enable real-world transfer from fast, rasterized simulation rendering. Under matched architectures, observations, and compute budgets, DRIFT outperforms two-phase distillation and achieves a 58.8% relative improvement in average real-world success rate across three manipulation tasks. Controlled simulation evaluations demonstrate that DRIFT's relative advantage grows from roughly 9% in-distribution to over 61% under out-of-distribution shifts, suggesting that stronger generalization contributes to superior real-world transfer. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [111] [Near-Zero Forgetting Is Not Enough: Out-of-Distribution Robustness in Continual Learning of Pretrained VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31511)
*Adrià Ruiz,Bartłomiej Twardowski,Tinne Tuytelaars,Sergi Masip*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究经验回放微调的Pi0 VLA模型在持续学习中虽保持域内近零遗忘，但对域偏移的鲁棒性严重不足，OOD旧任务性能会快速崩溃并伴随表征漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明预训练VLA在特定持续学习设置下经经验回放可实现近零遗忘，但其对已学任务域偏移的鲁棒性尚未被探索。

Method: 在LIBERO与LIBERO-Plus基准上，对使用经验回放微调的Pi0进行OOD评估，并结合表征分析与线性探针分析持续学习过程中的遗忘与特征漂移。

Result: 域内设置下灾难性遗忘接近零，但OOD旧任务性能迅速崩溃；OOD能力退化呈现跨域偏移共享的表征模式，并在两个较简单套件中表现为行为可见；表征漂移集中于多模态特征与动作特征；线性探针显示OOD特征仍保留任务相关信息，但似乎不可读，符合表征错位。

Conclusion: 即使域内稳定性保持完好，持续学习仍可能发生严重的OOD遗忘，并伴随表征漂移；因此评估VLA持续学习需同时关注域内稳定性与OOD鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent work shows that pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exhibit near-zero forgetting in specific continual learning settings with experience replay. However, robustness to domain shift of learned tasks remains unexplored. In this work, we study the robustness of Pi0 fine-tuned with experience replay under out-of-distribution (OOD) data using the LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus benchmarks. While the in-domain (ID) setup achieves near-zero catastrophic forgetting, OOD performance on previous tasks quickly collapses. Specifically, we show that during the continual learning process, the OOD capabilities of the model degrade, following a shared representational pattern across domain shifts, which is also visible behaviorally in the two simpler suites. Representational analysis shows that drift concentrates in the multimodal and action features. Linear probes indicate that OOD features still retain task-relevant information, but appear unreadable, consistent with representation misalignment. These findings suggest that even when ID stability remains intact, severe OOD forgetting can still occur, accompanied by representational drift during continual learning.

</details>
